TL;DR: MultiSynt/MT是一个开源多语言合成语料库，覆盖36种欧洲语言，含约4.8万亿目标语言token，通过翻译高质量英语语料生成。它在多语言LLM预训练中显著减少token需求（约72%更少），并提升性能，同时揭示现有评估方法的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放网络规模的预训练语料库高度集中于英语，限制了多语言大语言模型（LLM）的发展，尤其对中等和低资源欧洲语言的支持不足。

Method: 通过翻译1000亿高质量的Nemotron-CC英语token，使用Tower+和OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT翻译系统，生成了MultiSynt/MT合成平行语料库，覆盖36种欧洲语言，提供约4.8万亿目标语言token。

Result: 基于MultiSynt/MT训练的参考LLM在多语言基准测试中达到原生数据基线（HPLT 2.0）的最终分数，但仅用约72%更少的预训练token；在匹配1000亿token训练预算下，相对性能提升约15%。分析发现，标准多选题基准无法捕捉翻译质量差异，而流畅度敏感的LLM-as-judge评估能有效识别（MultiSynt本身无流畅度缺陷）；挪威语习语和文化任务仍以原生数据表现更优。

Conclusion: MultiSynt/MT为多语言预训练提供了大规模开源资源，显著提升效率和性能，尤其惠及中等和低资源语言；同时强调改进评估方法的重要性。语料库已发布（含多系统行对齐翻译），以推动受控的学术研究。

Abstract: Open web-scale pre-training corpora remain concentrated in English, limiting multilingual LLM development. We introduce MultiSynt/MT, an open synthetic parallel corpus with approximately 4.8 trillion target-language tokens across 36 European languages, produced by translating 100 billion high-quality Nemotron-CC tokens with Tower+ and OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT systems. For many medium- and lower-resource European languages, this is the largest openly available pre-training resource. On a broad multilingual benchmark suite, reference LLMs trained on MultiSynt/MT reach the final score of HPLT 2.0, a native-data baseline, using roughly 72% fewer pre-training tokens, and outperform it by approximately 15% relative at a matched 100B-token training budget. Our analyses also identify evaluation blind spots: standard multiple-choice benchmarks miss translation-quality differences that a fluency-sensitive LLM-as-judge evaluation cleanly recovers on the trained LLMs (with no fluency deficit in MultiSynt itself), and Norwegian idiomatic and culturally grounded tasks remain better served by native data. We release the corpus, including row-aligned translations from multiple systems, to support controlled research on multilingual pre-training data and evaluation.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Document Grounding: Span-Level Hallucination Detection over Code, Tool Output, and Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00895)
*Ádám Kovács,Bowei He,Xue Liu,István Boros,Szilveszter Tóth,Gábor Recski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个统一基准，涵盖代码、工具输出等结构化输入与自然语言文档的跨模态幻觉检测，微调Qwen3.5-2B模型在结构化数据上显著优于现有方法（如LettuceDetect-large），同时保持自然语言任务竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG幻觉检测研究集中于自然语言文档，但随着生成系统日益依赖代码、工具输出、Markdown表格等结构化输入，亟需构建覆盖多模态数据的统一评测框架。

Method: 从基于证据的正确回答出发，通过注入带精确字符标注的局部幻觉构建数据集，对代码测试集进行基于证据的审查验证，并采用微调策略优化Qwen3.5-2B检测器。

Result: 在统一测试集达到0.689 span-F1，在代码代理源上达0.60（LettuceDetect-large仅0.17，零样本LLM最高0.22）；同时保持自然语言基准竞争力（RAGTruth示例F1=81.8%，英语PsiloQA IoU=0.724）。

Conclusion: 结构化输入幻觉检测可通过专用基准和微调实现突破，本文方法显著优于现有技术在代码相关任务，同时验证了跨模态鲁棒性。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is usually evaluated on natural-language document evidence. However, grounded generation systems increasingly rely on structured inputs: source code, developer-tool output, markdown documents, tables, and repository metadata. We introduce a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection over code, tool output, structured documents, and existing natural-language RAG datasets. The benchmark is built by starting from grounded correct answers, injecting localized hallucinations with exact character labels, and validating the code test split with evidence-based review. Our fine-tuned Qwen3.5-2B detector reaches 0.689 span-F1 on the unified test set and 0.60 on the code-agent source, where it substantially outperforms LettuceDetect-large (0.17) and the strongest zero-shot LLM judges we evaluated (at most 0.22). The same model remains competitive on established natural-language benchmarks, with 81.8 RAGTruth example-F1 and 0.724 English PsiloQA IoU.

</details>


### [185] [From Personas to Plot: Character-Grounded Multi-Agent Story Generation for Long-Form Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00918)
*Aayush Aluru,Chloe Ho,Muhammad Hammouri,Kerry Luo,Myra Malik,Ryan Lagasse,Arjun Bahuguna,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAGNET框架通过多智能体生成和ATLAS验证，显著提升了长篇叙事的连贯性，减少幻觉达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成长篇故事时面临叙事一致性和情节连贯性问题。

Method: 提出MAGNET（多智能体目标驱动叙事引擎）和ATLAS（基于图的幻觉检测管道），结合LLM编辑和成对评分进行评估。

Result: 在100页故事中，MAGNET相比单模型基线减少幻觉41%和注释50%，相比IBSEN减少幻觉34%和注释45%；成对评分显示类似优势。

Conclusion: 显式世界状态跟踪和目标驱动的多智能体生成能涌现连贯长篇叙事，为可控生成提供基础。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive creative fiction generation, they struggle to maintain narrative consistency and coherent plot lines in long-form stories. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for long-form narrative generation and verification. MAGNET, a multi-agent goal-driven narrative engine for storytelling, generates stories with persona-grounded character agents that propose actions based on a shared world state and evolving story goals, while ATLAS is a graph-based pipeline that compares scene-level world representations across a generated story to detect hallucinations. By evaluating MAGNET using an LLM editor, pairwise rubric scoring, and ATLAS, we show that our framework produces coherent narratives compared to single-model prompting and IBSEN. At 100 pages, MAGNET reduced annotations and hallucinations by 41 and 50%, respectively, compared to the single model baseline and by 34 and 45%, respectively, compared to IBSEN, with pairwise rubric evaluation showing similar results. These results suggest that long-form narratives can emerge from explicit world-state tracking and goal-driven multi-agent generation, providing a foundation for controllable and structurally coherent long-form narrative generation.

</details>


### [186] [Persona Non Grata: LLM Persona-Driven Generations in MCQA are Unstable in Distinct Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00937)
*César Guerra-Solano,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了人格驱动的生成（PDGs）在多项选择题回答（MCQA）任务中的不稳定性问题，通过开发多维度指标发现不稳定性受模型特性、任务领域及提示格式影响，并强调检查超参数不稳定性的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文本对话中的人格稳定性研究较多，但非文本密集型输出（如MCQA）中的人格生成不稳定性被忽视，需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 开发了三个指标分别评估性能、结果和问题正确性的稳定性，从三个维度系统性分析MCQA任务中的人格生成不稳定性。

Result: 不稳定性在模型家族/规模和数学/常识问题领域呈现规律性差异；任务提示格式比温度等超参数引发更显著的不稳定性；不稳定性与任务准确性相关，且相似人格在不同实验设置下表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 揭示了超参数不稳定性对人格驱动生成的关键影响，建议在实际应用中严格评估此类不稳定性以确保任务可靠性。

Abstract: Persona-driven generations (PDGs) have seen prolific use in research and industry applications, where a large language model (LLM) takes on a 'persona' while completing some task. While persona expressed through free-form text (like dialogue) has substantial work investigating stability or consistency, relatively, persona expressed in non-text-heavy outputs (like in multiple-choice question answering, or MCQA) is often overlooked. We work to address this gap, seeking to understand the instability of LLM PDGs in MCQA tasks. We develop three metrics investigating the performance, outcome, and question correctness stability, evaluating three distinct dimensions. Using these metrics, we find that instability varies consistently between model families and model size, and across question domains, with math/commonsense questions leading to greater instability. We also find task prompt format introduces more prediction instability than other hyperparameters, like temperature. Finally, we find that instability is related to task accuracy, and using our instability metrics, find different experimental settings that result in different best and worst personas for tasks, despite their similarity. This reveals the importance of checking hyperparameter instability in PDGs.

</details>


### [187] [Quantifying the Affective Gap: A Zero-Shot Evaluation of LLMs on Fine-Grained Emotion Taxonomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00968)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Jessica M. Johnson,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了Claude、ChatGPT和Gemini三个商业大语言模型在零样本情感识别任务中的表现，发现Gemini表现最佳，所有模型在细粒度情感分类上均存在明显局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言情感识别是情感计算的基础挑战，对机交互、心理健康支持和对话AI具有重要意义。需要评估商业大语言模型在零样本情感识别方面的性能。

Method: 对三个主流商业大语言模型（Claude claude-sonnet-4-6、ChatGPT GPT-5.4、Gemini gemini-2.5-flash）进行统一的零样本评估。通过生产API查询，使用细粒度13类情感分类任务。采用boltuix/emotions数据集的1000句分层样本，对所有模型应用相同的无示例提示。

Result: Gemini表现最佳（准确率39.9%，macro-F1=0.363），GPT-5.4次之（准确率38.8%，macro-F1=0.291），Claude最低（准确率38.0%，macro-F1=0.159）。所有模型在讽刺和欲望类别表现好，但在爱、困惑和羞耻类别表现差。McNemar检验显示成对差异无统计显著性（p > 0.10）。Claude的宏观F1分数明显偏低，暴露了类别不平衡预测偏差。

Conclusion: 当前前沿AI系统在零样本细粒度情感分类方面存在明显局限性，模型似乎达到了共同的零样本上限，需要进一步研究解决类别不平衡问题。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in natural language is a foundational challenge in affective computing, with critical implications for human-computer interaction, mental health support, and conversational AI. This paper presents a rigorous, unified zero-shot evaluation of three leading commercial large language models: Claude (claude-sonnet-4-6), ChatGPT (GPT-5.4), and Gemini (gemini-2.5-flash). The models were queried through their respective production APIs as of April 2026 on a fine-grained 13-class emotion classification task. Using a stratified 1,000-sentence sample from the boltuix/emotions dataset, which comprises 131,306 sentences across 13 categories, a single uniform prompt with no exemplars was applied identically across all models. Gemini achieves the highest accuracy (39.9%) and macro-F1 score (0.363), followed by GPT-5.4 (38.8%, macro-F1 = 0.291) and Claude (38.0%, macro-F1 = 0.159). All models excel on sarcasm and desire while consistently failing on love, confusion, and shame. McNemar tests reveal no statistically significant pairwise differences (p > 0.10), suggesting convergence at a shared zero-shot ceiling. Claude's markedly lower macro-F1 score exposes a class-imbalance prediction bias. These findings highlight the current limitations of frontier AI systems in zero-shot fine-grained emotion classification.

</details>


### [188] [Svarna: An Open Corpus Workbench for Modern Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00970)
*Stergios Chatzikyriakidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Svarna是一个免费的、开源的、基于网络的现代希腊语语料库工作平台，整合了5个数据库，提供超过5.07亿单词和约2900万句子的语料资源，填补了希腊语技术领域的资源分散和访问限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希腊语言技术存在长期缺口，现有语料库资源分散在不同平台，许多情况下机构访问受限或已无法在线获取，缺乏统一、易用的语料库工具。

Method: 系统基于SQLite FTS5全文索引技术，通过FastAPI后端提供服务，作为Docker容器部署在Azure云端，采用MIT许可证开源发布，支持用户添加自定义语料库和部署实例。

Result: 成功整合了5个涵盖不同语域、机构、文学、方言、社交媒体和历史语料的数据库，提供50700万单词和2900万句子的语料资源；实现了KWIC标记、频率分析、搭配提取、话语标记词典、n-gram分析、语域比较、正则表达式搜索等多种功能，并支持LLM层面的语用标注。

Conclusion: Svarna作为探索可用数据的第一步，解决了希腊语语料库资源的分散性问题，为未来的希腊语言技术研究奠定了坚实基础，预期将推动更全面的研究发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces Svarna, a free, open-source, web-based corpus workbench for modern Greek. Svarna integrates five databases covering various registers, institutional, literary, dialectal, social media, and historical, to provide a total of more than 507 million words and around 29 million sentences. This platform addresses the chronic gaps in Greek language technology. Although various corpus resources exist, they are scattered across different platforms, and in many cases, institutional access is restricted or they are no longer available online. Svarna integrates these resources into a single interface that can be used without logging in, installation, or specialized training. This system provides a concordancer with KWIC marking capabilities, frequency analysis including register-by-register normalization, collocation extraction using mutual information, a dictionary of 93 Greek discourse markers providing distribution profiles, text-level analysis tools including n-grams, variants, and collocation networks, register comparison using log-ratio, regular expression search, and an optional LLM layer for pragmatic annotation and free research mode. This platform is built upon SQLite FTS5 full-text indexes provided via a FastAPI backend, deployed as Docker containers on Azure, and released under the MIT license. Source code, build scripts, and deployment configurations are publicly available on GitHub. Users can add their own corpora and deploy their own instances. This document describes the system design, corpus structure, and use cases demonstrating the various queries supported by the platform. Svarna serves as the first step in exploring available data and is expected to lay the foundation for more comprehensive research in the future.

</details>


### [189] [KnowledgeDebugger -- an Exploration Tool for Knowledge Localization and Editing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01000)
*Eric Benz,Lennart Stöpler,Nikolai Bolik,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KnowledgeDebugger，一款基于GUI的无代码工具，支持Transformer知识定位与编辑的快速探索，通过整合EasyEdit库方法并验证其有效性，助力研究人员高效发现知识编辑现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer知识编辑研究需经历单样本现象探索与统计验证两阶段，但缺乏专为初期探索设计的工具，导致实验效率受限。

Method: 开发KnowledgeDebugger GUI工具，整合EasyEdit库的先进知识编辑方法，提供零编程门槛的操作界面，实现模型内部知识的可视化定位与实时编辑实验。

Result: 通过多个知识编辑领域案例研究验证：该工具能显著提升探索效率，快速复现并扩展已有发现，为后续统计实验提供可靠假设基础。

Conclusion: KnowledgeDebugger填补了知识编辑研究初期的工具空白，推动Transformer知识机制的可解释性研究范式优化，具有显著的科研应用价值。

Abstract: Recent research has increasingly focused on understanding how Transformers store and process knowledge, as well as how this knowledge can be edited. Research work in this area is often conducted in two phases: first, phenomena are explored on individual samples. Then, when results appear promising, more statistically robust experiments follow. To support the first phase, we propose KnowledgeDebugger, a GUI-based exploration tool for knowledge localization and editing in Transformers. Our tool - inspired by LM-Debugger - offers no-code access to the methods in EasyEdit, a widely used library of state-of-the-art Knowledge Editing approaches. We demonstrate the tool's effectiveness through case studies of recent findings in this field.

</details>


### [190] [Logit-Contribution Scoring Identifies Non-Literal Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01002)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Beatrice Alex,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS)方法，有效识别大型语言模型中执行非字面检索的注意力头。通过消融实验证明，LOCOS检测的关键头对长上下文任务至关重要，其重要性远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力头检测器基于"字面复制"标准（关注标记与生成标记匹配），仅能识别"读取"位置，无法捕捉通过输出值(OV)电路实现非字面信息合成的"写入"机制，导致遗漏关键注意力头。

Method: 提出Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS)，通过计算各头OV电路输出在答案标记解嵌入方向上的投影值进行评分，在单次前向传播中对比针(target)与目标源(position)位置，实现写感知检测。

Result: 在Qwen3/Gemma-3/OLMo-3.1模型上验证：① LOCOS头部消融在NoLiMa基准上比现有方法更早导致ROUGE-L崩溃(Qwen3-8B消融50头时ROUGE-L从0.401→0.000，基线法仍保持0.292)；② 所选头具有任务特异性(参数记忆/算术推理不受影响)；③ 消融显著降低MuSiQue(0.55→0.08)和BABI-Long(0.62→0.20)性能，随机头控制仅波动±0.05。

Conclusion: LOCOS成功解决现有检测方法无法捕获非字面检索机制的缺陷，证实特定注意力头对长上下文信息合成具有决定性作用，为模型行为解释提供了新工具。

Abstract: In long-context use, large language models frequently synthesize answers from the meaning of a relevant context span rather than literally copy-pasting them. Identifying which attention heads perform this synthesis matters for interpreting long-context model behavior. Yet existing detectors miss these heads by construction: they reward heads whose attended token matches the generated token, a literal-copy criterion that captures where a head reads but not what it writes through its output-value (OV) circuit, the very mechanism that carries non-literal retrieval. We introduce Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS), a write-aware detector that scores each head by the projection of its OV-circuit output onto the answer-token unembedding direction, contrasting needle and off-needle source positions in a single forward pass. Across three model families (Qwen3, Gemma-3, OLMo-3.1), mean-ablating the top LOCOS heads on the NoLiMa non-literal retrieval benchmark collapses ROUGE-L at lower head counts than prior attention-based detections; on Qwen3-8B, ablating 50 heads drives ROUGE-L from 0.401 to 0.000 while the strongest baseline still retains 0.292. The selected heads are retrieval-specific: parametric recall and arithmetic reasoning stay at baseline under the same ablation. On Qwen3-8B, the same ablation also drops MuSiQue from 0.55 to 0.08 and BABI-Long from 0.62 to 0.20, while a random-heads control stays within 0.05 of baseline.

</details>


### [191] [Understanding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01006)
*Yannik Keller,Thomas Eisenmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章节系统梳理了大型语言模型（LLMs）的核心技术原理、认知能力特征及人机认知差异，批判简单化归因LLM行为的学术观点，呼吁基于实证开展 nuanced 的AI认知讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决对LLMs运行机制、能力边界及其与人类认知关系的核心争议，建立客观认知框架

Method: 1) 解析Transformer架构与注意力机制的技术基础；2) 通过实证研究分析符号推理/心理理论等涌现能力；3) 对比人类与LLM的认知失效案例；4) 综述可解释AI技术路径

Result: 证实LLMs在特定任务中展现近似人类认知的能力，同时揭示其与人类思维的本质差异；发现现有反拟人化论点存在优化过程误判和能力简化问题

Conclusion: 反对用"模式记忆"简单否定AI认知可能性，主张摒弃二元对立思维，在承认人机差异的前提下探索AI认知的合理性

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) represent one of the most significant advances in AI and natural language processing in recent years. Still, many pressing questions about their mechanisms, capabilities, and relationship to human cognition remain highly debated. This chapter aims to outline our current understanding of LLMs by discussing recent evidence on emerging capabilities and their mechanistic implementation within processing layers. We begin with a concise overview of the Transformer architecture, emphasizing how the attention mechanism enables training on massive datasets, allowing LLMs to function as generalist rather than specialized models. Next, we examine emergent LLM capabilities that appear to resemble aspects of human cognition, including symbolic reasoning, theory of mind, and deception strategies. Several studies provide evidence that LLMs can solve tasks previously thought to require human-like cognition. Other studies reveal insightful failure cases that shed light on the differences between human and LLM cognition. Alongside these findings, we review explainable AI approaches ranging from neuron activation analysis to circuit tracing. In the final section, we address current debates concerning what LLMs genuinely understand versus what they merely appear to understand. Prominent arguments against AI anthropomorphism point to the simplicity of LLM training objectives, claiming that LLM behavior is better explained by pattern memorization of training data than by genuine cognition. We argue that this standpoint is guided by misconceptions about optimization processes and cognitive capacity, and advocate for a more nuanced discussion of LLM cognition that neither dismisses the differences between humans and LLMs nor precludes the possibility of AI cognition through overly simplistic reductionist arguments.

</details>


### [192] [Reading Order Inference for Complex Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01018)
*Iddo Hakim,Sharva Gogawale,Omer Ventura,Gal Grudka,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的图框架，将OCR文本行作为节点，通过结合因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测信号的加权集成对边进行评分，并利用最大后悔推断规则恢复全局阅读顺序。在复杂历史手稿（如Glossa Ordinaria）和多栏文档上表现优异，优于XY-cut和LayoutReader。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读顺序推断是复杂历史手稿数字化的关键瓶颈，尤其是具有多空间交错阅读流的页面（如Glossa Ordinaria布局）。现有方法如递归XY-cut和LayoutReader存在误差累积、泛化性差等问题，特别是在镜像、翻转等几何变化下性能下降显著。

Method: 提出基于图的框架：每个OCR文本行作为节点构建有向候选转换图；边由因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测信号的加权加性集成评分；使用度约束有向路径覆盖恢复阅读顺序；提出最大后悔推断规则优先选择高机会成本的边，避免贪婪边选择的级联失败。

Result: 在合成Glossa网格布局、23个ALTO页面几何和140页OmniDocBench多栏子集上评估。在Glossa布局上平均恢复95%的真值后继边，XY-cut为50%；在OmniDocBench上达到88%宏边准确率，XY-cut为75%，LayoutReader为25%。镜像不变性验证显示方法变化小于1个百分点，而XY-cut变化2个百分点，LayoutReader-T高达8个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提方法在复杂阅读顺序推断任务上显著提升性能，具有无需训练、计算高效和强鲁棒性，为历史手稿数字化提供了有效解决方案，并克服了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Reading order inference remains a critical bottleneck in the digitization of complex historical manuscripts, where pages contain multiple spatially interleaved reading streams, the canonical example being the Glossa Ordinaria layout, in which a central text is surrounded by commentaries that wrap around it in non-rectangular, non-convex regions. We present a training-free, graph-based framework: each OCR text line becomes a node in a directed candidate-transition graph, edges are scored by a weighted additive ensemble of two lightweight language-model signals (causal language model conditional likelihood and BERT next-sentence prediction, NSP; a third sentence-embedding signal was evaluated but did not improve reading order), and the global reading order is recovered as a degree-constrained directed path cover. To avoid the cascading "edge-theft" failures of greedy edge selection, we propose a max-regret inference rule that prioritizes commitments with high opportunity cost. We evaluate on synthetic Glossa Ordinaria grid layouts, on 23 ALTO page geometries (10 historical source pages plus mirrored and flipped variants), and on a 140-page multi-column English subset of OmniDocBench, comparing our method against the canonical recursive XY-cut (PaddleOCR PP-StructureV3) and two LayoutReader variants (layout-only and text+layout) on identical inputs. On wrap-around Glossa layouts our method recovers 95% of ground-truth successor edges on average vs. XY-cut's 50%; on the OmniDocBench multi-column subset it reaches 88% macro edge accuracy versus XY-cut's 75% and LayoutReader's 25%. The LayoutReader baselines transfer poorly due to a word-level vs. line-level granularity mismatch. We additionally verify mirror-invariance under horizontal and vertical page reflections: Our method changes by less than 1 percentage point, classical XY-cut by 2 points, and LayoutReader-T by up to 8 points.

</details>


### [193] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinKG-News框架，通过构建基于新闻事件和公司知识图谱，结合上下文学习生成信用风险报告，在提升生成质量的同时显著减少幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场动态受新闻事件影响，但现有方法难以显式建模事件与市场间的隐含关联，亟需事实性、公司中心化的环境感知知识图谱来增强解释能力。

Method: 开发FinKG-News框架自动抽取新闻事件作为锚点并关联至公司，构建结构化知识图谱；基于该图谱设计上下文学习架构，整合事件-新闻-公司数据进行三维财务分析。

Result: 实验表明自动幻觉检测不可靠，需依赖专家评估；所提方法较基准模型生成质量提升19%-34%，幻觉率显著降低。

Conclusion: 首次实现新闻事件驱动的知识图谱辅助信用风险报告生成，验证了显式事件建模的有效性，并通过开源代码促进领域研究。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [194] [Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01034)
*Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出动态人格框架，通过联合调节AI代理的隐喻性角色（如教练、导师等）与性格表达强度，解决现有对话代理在医疗、健身等场景中因固定人格导致的适配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话代理普遍存在人格与风格固化问题，在任务复杂度、紧急性、正式性变化时易产生不匹配；现有研究表明中等性格强度和上下文相关隐喻角色优于极端或静态设计，但尚未有系统性动态调节方案。

Method: 构建Fluid Personality Framework，实现双重动态调节机制：(1) 根据任务上下文切换隐喻性角色类型；(2) 基于用户目标/特征及情境紧急度调整性格表达强度（低/中/高三级）。

Result: 理论上建立首个整合角色转换与强度调节的框架体系，为跨场景适配提供设计维度规范，但尚未提供实验验证数据。

Conclusion: 该框架可显著提升对话代理在动态交互中的情境契合度，为医疗咨询、健身指导等需灵活人格调整的应用领域提供新范式，后续需开展实证研究验证有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

</details>


### [195] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于代理式LLM群体构建可解释的人工生命计算平台，通过自然语言交互实现复杂行为的涌现与直接可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工生命研究面临复杂性与可解释性不可兼得的困境：复杂系统难以解释，简单系统无法产生涌现行为。单个LLM缺乏生命特征，但群体交互可突破此限制。

Method: 构建具备持久记忆、工具调用能力和自主行动力的代理式LLM群体，利用自然语言作为通信协议，建立可观测的文本行为轨迹分析框架。

Result: 在受控实验和真实部署场景中验证了代理集合的涌现动态特性，证明其能产生超越单体模型的新行为模式，同时保持对研究者的可解释性。

Conclusion: 代理式LLM群体为人工生命研究提供了兼具复杂性与可解释性的新计算范式，可通过自然语言直接 interrogate 集体行为，开辟了可解释涌现系统研究的新方向。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [196] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出消息传递语言模型（MPLM）框架，通过线程间直接通信优化大语言模型的并行推理效率，显著降低计算成本并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统链式思考（CoT）方法存在计算瓶颈，而并行分叉-合并（FJ）范式因线程无法点对点通信导致可扩展性受限。

Method: MPLM采用轻量级发送/接收原语实现线程间直接通信，并通过减少冗余上下文共享和基于部分信息的提前终止机制提升效率。

Result: 在数独任务中实现渐近更小的上下文需求，解决25×25难题；在3-SAT任务中通过抢占提升效率；在长上下文问答中与主流FJ方法表现相当。

Conclusion: MPLM通过高效通信机制突破现有并行推理瓶颈，为可扩展的大语言模型推理提供新范式。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [197] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedQADE，首个德语临床开放回答评估基准，验证了自动化LLM评判者虽在统计对齐度上接近医生，但仍缺乏临床元认知与独立性的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放答案评估具更高临床有效性，但人工评分效率低。现有自动化LLM评判者能否复制临床校准与谨慎性尚未验证，需建立标准化评估体系。

Method: 构建含3,800道题的MedQADE基准，由10名执业医生和9个LLM共同标注。采用Kappa系数量化评估者间一致性，并通过难度分层分析临床元认知特征。

Result: 1. 最佳模型(Gemini 3 Flash)与医生κ值达0.694(医生天花板0.709)；2. 自动化评判者无临床元认知，始终给出确定性评分；3. 发现模型对自身架构衍生品的系统性偏好，该偏差不受语言影响。

Conclusion: 统计对齐度不等同临床适用性，自动化评判需具备难度自适应的谨慎机制，且必须通过独立架构验证以确保评估客观性。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [198] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种多模态大学聊天机器人，结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术，通过整合大语言模型与语义检索系统，提供基于机构文档的智能问答服务。系统支持文本和图像输入，在降低幻觉率（31.7%→6.6%）的同时保持高用户满意度，为发展中国家高校提供可部署于受限硬件的实用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家高校普遍缺乏智能支持系统，现有基于规则的聊天机器人无法有效处理复杂领域查询和动态政策更新，导致利益相关者信息获取困难

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）架构，结合视觉语言模型实现多模态输入处理；集成FastAPI后端与Next.js前端构建可扩展系统；通过量化推理优化在受限硬件上的部署效率

Result: 多模态评估显示：1) 文本/图像查询均维持高用户满意度 2) 视觉输入响应时间增加但可接受 3) 幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6% 4) 检索 grounding 显著提升回答准确性

Conclusion: 该系统成功填补了高校智能支持系统的空白，通过RAG技术有效平衡准确性与实用性，在降低幻觉的同时满足多模态交互需求，为发展中国家教育信息化提供可靠技术路径

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [199] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出对数量化新方法，提升4位精度性能，节省内存并加速，适合消费GPU部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均匀量化方法在处理低频率高幅度权重时易产生次优表示，难以满足消费级硬件部署需求，亟需更高效的量化方案。

Method: 提出Log$_\text{b}$Quant，采用可调底数的对数量化策略，针对参数分布特性优化量化过程，以适配常见模型参数的分布特征。

Result: 在4位精度下，Log$_\text{b}$Quant在多个基准测试中显著优于张量粒度非对称线性量化，同时实现中等速度提升与高内存节省率。

Conclusion: Log$_\text{b}$Quant通过可调底数的对数量化方法，有效解决了传统均匀量化在高幅度低频率权重下的性能瓶颈，在消费级GPU上实现了高性能与低资源占用的平衡。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [200] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了首个通用AI创造力基准AGC-Bench（含78个数据集），开发AGC-Judge开源评分模型，发现大语言模型存在可测量的创造力因子'c'（类似智力因子'g'），且创造力表现与领域相关，但顶尖模型仍落后于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI创造力研究缺乏统一评估基准，且存在两个核心争议：创造力是否具有领域特异性？创造力是否能与通用智力分离？这些问题亟需系统性验证。

Method: 1. 通过系统综述筛选3,101篇论文、497个现有基准，构建跨7大领域的AGC-Bench数据集
2. 开发代理框架将异构代码库转换为HELM标准化格式
3. 基于法官响应理论校准LLM评分偏差，用三巨头模型微调Qwen3-30B生成AGC-Judge
4. 对83个LLM进行因子分析，对比人类匹配子集表现

Result: 发现1：因子分析揭示创造力存在单一主因子'c'（解释81.5%方差），与通用知识/推理相关但可分离
发现2：显式提示'要有创造力'比启用推理机制更能提升表现，证明基准有效测量创造力而非通用能力
发现3：顶尖人类在匹配测试中仍超越最强LLM

Conclusion: 本研究建立了首个标准化AI创造力评估体系，证实创造力可量化且具领域差异性，为AI创造力研究提供开源基础设施。关键突破在于通过心理测量学方法解决LLM评判偏差，并首次发现创造力因子'c'的存在。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [201] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对现有语言模型安全评估依赖模糊自然语言行为判断、既有基准简单归类为通过/失败的缺陷，提出对抗语用学基准与标注协议，配套语言学分类体系、种子基准、专家评估流程和专项指标，可将语言学判断方法转化为实用工具，应用于安全评估验证、LLM裁判优化、提示注入测试等多个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型安全评估广泛依赖对模糊自然语言行为的判断，包括指令遵循情况、策略合规性、命令抵抗效果等，但现有基准普遍将复杂判断压缩为二分类通过/失败标签，无法区分失败究竟是模型能力不足、策略本身存在模糊性、指令冲突、辅助框架故障还是评估者判断不稳定导致，缺乏细化的诊断能力。

Method: 提出对抗语用学框架，包含四个核心组成部分：1）受语言学规范约束的分类体系，覆盖指令冲突、嵌套命令、引用歧义、指示代词歧义、间接言语行为、多轮代理任务等场景；2）带验证者强制元数据的18项种子基准；3）54行的本地种子试点；4）专家评估协议，可区分任务成功度、策略合规性、安全风险等级、拒绝结果、评估者置信度五个维度，同时配套裁判有效性、诊断模糊性、分类体系漂移三个专项指标。

Result: 该框架成功将语言学领域的主观判断方法论转化为可落地的实用工具，可支撑安全评估验证、LLM裁判可靠性验证、金标准数据集构建、提示注入攻击测试、安全文档完善等多个安全评估相关场景。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的对抗语用学评估框架突破了现有语言模型安全评估只能做二分类判断的局限，可精准归因模型在模糊语言场景下的行为问题来源，为后续语言模型安全评估标准化、评估工具优化、对抗攻击防御提供了方法论和工具层面的支撑。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [202] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Distill to Detect (D2D)方法，通过将可疑语言模型与基础模型间的分布差异蒸馏至KV-cache前缀适配器，放大隐藏偏好偏差信号，实现可检测性。实验验证其有效性和理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景中的语言模型可能存在隐蔽偏好偏差，且防御方因未知偏差主题难以检测，现有方法无法可靠发现仅作用于特定主题的隐藏行为。

Method: D2D利用前缀调整的容量瓶颈，通过KV-cache前缀适配器蒸馏模型间logit分布差异，将隐藏偏差转化为生成文本中可观测信号。

Result: D2D成功放大多种类型隐藏偏见至可检测水平，理论框架通过Fisher权重投影解释logit分布变化有效性，并获实证支持。

Conclusion: D2D为审计部署语言模型中的隐蔽行为提供了实用工具，解决了防御方的检测不对称性问题。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [203] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出状态-预测分离假设，设计双计算流Transformer变体，通过分离状态存储与token预测功能，显著提升数据效率、计算效率及下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer将状态存储与token预测耦合在同一计算流中，可能导致资源利用不充分和性能瓶颈，需要探索功能解耦的优化方案。

Method: 设计双计算流Transformer架构，独立处理状态存储与token预测；在多种规模下进行预训练实验，并通过梯度分析验证设计有效性。

Result: 实验表明分离设计在验证损失上持续优于标准Transformer，数据/计算效率提升，下游任务性能平均提高2-3个百分点；梯度分析证实其机制差异。

Conclusion: 状态-预测分离是提升语言模型性能的有效途径，其梯度特性差异为理论创新提供实证支持。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [204] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过大规模评估框架发现，LLMs生成的科研想法虽然在数量上可观，但在分布特征上与人类研究者存在系统性偏差，范围更窄且集中在特定模式上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM想法评估主要关注单个想法的新颖性、可行性或专家偏好，缺乏对LLM生成想法与人类研究者想法差距的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了大规模想法评估框架，从高质量人类研究论文中逆向工程出相关先前工作集，让LLMs基于论文标题和摘要生成新想法；引入双轴研究品味分类法（机会模式和研究范式）来量化人类与LLM想法的分歧。

Result: 在不同LLMs生成的想法集中观察到一致的分布差距：LLM想法过度集中在桥接式机会和综合方法上，而人类论文参考分布在框架差距和构建贡献方面更加广泛。

Conclusion: 强大的LLMs可以生成一系列合理的科研想法，但其想法范围比人类研究者的品味更窄，且存在系统性偏差。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [MeshDNS: A Cooperative DNS Resolution Framework for Resource-Constrained IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00122)
*Asif Mahbub,Md. Abir Hossain,Nabil Bin Hannan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MeshDNS is a decentralized, secure DNS resolution framework for resource-constrained IoT networks using hash-based cache summaries and Ed25519-signed quorum voting to resolve cold-cache misses, achieving sub-50ms warm-cache performance and Byzantine fault isolation with minimal hardware overhead.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional centralized DNS systems are unsuitable for IoT environments due to resource constraints (e.g., sub-50KB RAM), unreliable connectivity, and security vulnerabilities creating single points of failure. The paper addresses these challenges by designing a cooperative resolution framework resilient to network churn and Byzantine faults under shared-key admission models.

Method: MeshDNS implements: (1) decentralized architecture with nodes maintaining hash-based cache summaries for awareness; (2) Ed25519 digital signatures and identical-answer quorum voting to secure cold-cache resolution; (3) shared-key admission control to limit threats to admitted peers. The system trades cryptographic latency (~1.3-1.7s) for Byzantine fault isolation during initial resolution.

Result: On ESP8266 microcontrollers (80MHz, <50KB RAM): Warm-cache resolution achieved 0.47ms vs mDNS's 1.39ms. Cold-cache misses incurred predictable cryptographic delays but successfully isolated Byzantine faults. Validation via 5-node physical testbed and 1,000-node simulations demonstrated resilient local caches under network churn.

Conclusion: MeshDNS provides a viable solution for secure DNS resolution in constrained IoT deployments, balancing performance and security through decentralized design. Its Byzantine fault tolerance and low warm-cache latency make it suitable for edge telemetry applications, with open-source implementation enabling further validation.

Abstract: Domain Name System (DNS) resolution in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to resource constraints, unreliable connectivity, and security vulnerabilities. Traditional centralized DNS architectures introduce single points of failure. This paper presents MeshDNS, a cooperative DNS resolution framework designed for resource-constrained IoT environments operating under shared-key admission. MeshDNS employs a decentralized architecture where nodes maintain cache awareness using hash-based summaries and secure cold-cache misses via Ed25519-signed, identical-answer quorum voting. Our implementation on commodity ESP8266 microcontrollers (sub-50 KB usable RAM, 80 MHz) achieves a 0.47 ms warm-cache resolution, outperforming native mDNS baselines (1.39 ms). To secure initial cold-cache misses, MeshDNS trades a predictable ~1.3-1.7s cryptographic penalty to successfully isolate Byzantine faults among admitted peers. Assuming a threat model where physical hardware extraction remains out of scope, MeshDNS demonstrates Byzantine fault isolation. We validated the framework via a 5-node physical testbed and discrete-event simulations scaling to 1,000 nodes; the results demonstrate that MeshDNS maintains resilient local name caches for persistent edge telemetry under network churn. Code is available at https://github.com/mahbubasif/MeshDNS-Artifact.

</details>


### [206] [An LLM-Based Framework for Intent-Driven Network Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00292)
*Kholoud El-Habbouli,Fen Zhou,Stephane Huet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型在从自然语言需求生成网络拓扑方面的能力，通过约束驱动的管道和系统验证框架，在四个现实网络场景中进行多模型比较分析，为AI驱动的网络设计提供系统性的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言需求设计可部署且具有弹性的网络拓扑是网络自动化领域的一个挑战性难题。需要探索大语言模型在处理结构性和弹性约束方面的能力，以支持AI驱动的网络设计决策。

Method: 提出了一个约束驱动的管道，结合分层建模和系统验证，评估专有和开源大语言模型的能力。通过多模型比较在四个现实网络场景中进行测试，使用节点和边F1分数评估结构正确性，通过服务器和内容连接性指标评估弹性。

Result: 分析了生成的拓扑结构中的常见故障模式，包括接口不匹配和方向不一致问题。通过定量评估指标比较了不同大语言模型在结构正确性和弹性方面的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解大语言模型在拓扑合成中处理结构和弹性约束的能力提供了系统性的基准，支持AI驱动网络设计中的明智模型选择。

Abstract: Designing deployable and resilient network topologies from natural language requirements remains a challenging problem in network automation. This work investigates the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate structurally valid and constraint-compliant network topologies through a constraint-driven pipeline combining hierarchical modeling and systematic validation. The framework is evaluated via a multimodel comparison of proprietary and open-weight LLMs across four realistic network scenarios released as a public dataset. We assess structural correctness using node and edge F1-scores against reference topologies, and evaluate resilience through server and content connectivity metrics. In addition, we analyze common failure modes, including interface mismatches and directional inconsistencies in generated topologies. Overall, this work provides a systematic benchmark for understanding how LLMs handle structural and resilience constraints in topology synthesis, and supports informed model selection for AI-driven network design.

</details>


### [207] [Robust Base Station Placement in Agricultural IoT via Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00549)
*Gourav Prateek Sharma,Durgesh Singh,James Gross*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对季节性植被变化的农业5G网络覆盖优化问题，提出基于高斯过程贝叶斯优化的最大最小季节覆盖方法，在1平方公里多作物农场以3个基站实现72.8%最差情况覆盖，较现有方法提升4.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业网络需应对作物生长导致的传播环境剧烈变化，传统基站部署方法难以保证全生长周期的可靠覆盖，且射线追踪模拟计算成本过高。

Method: 将基站部署建模为最大最小季节覆盖问题，采用高斯过程贝叶斯优化框架，利用射线追踪构建鲁棒目标的概率代理模型，减少计算开销。

Result: 在1平方公里多作物农场（3.5GHz频段）测试中，仅需3个基站即可实现72.8%最差情况覆盖，计算次数少于50次，所有季节阶段均优于现有方法4.6个百分点以上。

Conclusion: 该方法通过贝叶斯优化有效解决季节性覆盖优化问题，在保持低计算成本的同时显著提升覆盖可靠性，为精准农业网络部署提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Precision-agriculture networks based on private 5G NR should ensure reliable connectivity for IoT sensor nodes throughout the crop growing season, yet the propagation environment changes dramatically as vegetation grows and matures. We formulate $K$-base-station~(BS) placement as a \textit{maximin seasonal coverage} problem that maximizes the worst-case coverage fraction across all crop growth stages. Since each objective evaluation requires expensive ray-tracing simulations across all stages, we adopt a Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization~(GPBO) framework that builds a probabilistic surrogate of the robust objective using ray tracing. On a $1\,\text{km}^2$ multi-crop farm with three distinct crop zones at $3.5\,\text{GHz}$, the proposed scheme achieves $72.8\%$ worst-case coverage with $K{=}3$ BSs in fewer than fifty ray-tracing evaluations, outperforming budget-matched state-of-the-art approaches by at least $4.6\,\text{pp}$ across all four seasonal stages.

</details>


### [208] [Vehicle-to-Grid as a 5G Smart Grid Vertical: Non-Technical Barriers and Implications for Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00589)
*Shangqing Wang,Laura del Rio Carazo,Frank H. P. Fitzek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: V2G规模化部署受非技术障碍制约，需将社会、经济、治理因素转化为5G通信系统需求，推动多学科协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管V2G技术已成熟，但其大规模部署受限于商业、治理、社会和基础设施等非技术因素，亟需将这些约束整合到5G通信架构设计中。

Method: 采用PRISMA方法系统综述974篇文献，提炼162项实施关键研究，构建商业/经济、治理/政策、社会、基础设施四领域分析框架，并开展时空维度趋势研究。

Result: 2021年后研究重心转向多学科整合，非技术障碍占主导；提出精细化计量、ISO15118/OCPP协议互操作、隐私保护、低延迟高可靠服务、边云协同控制等通信需求。

Conclusion: 5G V2G系统设计必须超越纯技术优化，从初始阶段融合社会技术约束，推动可持续数据驱动的通信架构研究。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Grid (V2G) and broader Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) technologies are technically mature and widely demonstrated, yet large-scale deployment is constrained mainly by non-technical rather than communication or power-electronics limits. This paper targets the wireless communications community and frames V2G as a 5G-enabled smart grid vertical, linking business, governance, social, and infrastructure barriers to concrete communication-system requirements. Building on a PRISMA-guided systematic review of 974 V2G/V2X publications (2009-2025), and 162 implementation-critical studies, we adopt a four-domain framework of non-technical barriers: Business/Economic, Governance/Policy, Social, and Infrastructure/Ecosystem. Temporal and regional analyses show a shift from technical dominance to multidisciplinary integration after 2021. We translate these domains into communication requirements for V2G verticals, including fine-grained metering and settlement, protocol interoperability (e.g., ISO~15118, OCPP), privacy-by-design data governance, latency- and reliability-differentiated services, and edge-cloud partitioning for flexibility control. The results demonstrate that 5G design for V2G cannot be a purely technical optimization task and must integrate socio-technical constraints from the outset, suggesting research directions for sustainable, data-driven V2G communication architectures.

</details>


### [209] [Fluid-Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry: Information Flow in Non-Stationary Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00616)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Fluid-Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry (F-STSG) 框架，首次将动态网络拓扑建模为流体动力学极限，通过最优传输理论建立标量势场控制网络负载演化，揭示非平稳条件下信息流动的宏观规律及与运动学熵的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间网络理论依赖平稳点过程假设，无法刻画节点强度场在时空中的连续演化特性，导致对动态场景下信息流动限的分析失效。需构建能描述非平稳拓扑演化的统一理论框架。

Method: 创新性提出流体-时空随机几何(F-STSG)模型，将离散节点集合的演化视为流体动力学极限；通过最小动能原理建立逆边值问题，证明存在唯一标量势场控制网络负载压缩演化；耦合连续拉格朗日传输与离散欧拉干扰几何，推导出信息通量向量与物质导数作为关键统计量。

Result: 1) 建立标量势场唯一存在性定理，量化网络负载压缩演化；2) 证明信息通量向量是宏观平流的充分统计量；3) 物质导数可预测拓扑发散；4) 通过能量密度缩放与信源-信道对应关系，揭示协调开销、拓扑变形与控制信令需求与运动学熵的定量关联。

Conclusion: 首次为动态空间网络建立严格的场论表述框架，突破平稳假设局限，为非平稳网络的信息流动限提供解析工具，其运动学熵理论对自适应网络架构设计具有重要指导价值。

Abstract: The fundamental limits of information flow in spatial networks are usually characterized under stationary spatial point processes, but this assumption cannot capture non-stationary regimes where the node intensity field evolves continuously in space and time. This paper develops Fluid-Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry (F-STSG), treating dynamic network topology as a hydrodynamic limit of the discrete node constellation. We formulate the identification of latent network dynamics as an inverse boundary value problem and, using the minimum kinetic energy principle from optimal transport, establish the existence and uniqueness of a scalar potential field governing the compressive evolution of network load. The resulting field-theoretic formulation couples continuous Lagrangian transport with discrete Eulerian interference geometry. Based on this model, we derive the information flux vector as a sufficient statistic for macroscopic advection and the material derivative as a kinematic predictor of topological divergence. We further characterize non-stationary network limits through energy-density scaling and source-channel interpretation, showing how coordination overhead, topology deformation, and control signaling requirements are linked to the kinematic entropy of the evolving network topology.

</details>


### [210] [5G Configured Grant Scheduling for 5G-TSN Integration for the Support of Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00704)
*Ana Larrañaga,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Imanol Martinez,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对工业4.0环境下5G与TSN网络集成挑战，提出了一种新的5G配置授权(CG)调度方案，通过利用TSN提供的流量特征信息协调两个网络的调度决策，显著提升了系统可服务的TSN流量数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业4.0范式下，工厂向数字化数据驱动生态系统演进，需要实施更可靠、有弹性和定制化的制造系统。这些工业服务（如数字孪生、扩展现实、协作机器人）需要支持严格延迟和可靠性要求的高度可靠和确定性通信网络。5G网络具备满足这些要求的能力，但在工业环境中的有效应用需要与TSN标准有线技术进行高效集成。TSN提供前所未有的确定性服务级别和完美有界延迟，但实现5G与TSN网络集成的关键挑战在于协调两个网络的调度器。

Method: 本文提出了一种新型的5G配置授权(CG)调度方案，该方案集成到TSN网络中。所提出的方案利用TSN提供的TSN流量特征信息，协调其决策与TSN的调度。TSN拥有5G-TSN集成网络的能力信息，负责决定每个TSN流量流的路径和调度，而5G的调度必须根据TSN提供的调度决策和信息进行，以保证TSN流量的端到端延迟要求。

Result: 研究表明，所提出的调度方案显著增加了能够满足延迟要求的TSN流量数量，证明了该方案在提升工业网络集成性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了所提出的5G配置授权调度方案能够有效解决工业4.0环境中5G与TSN网络集成的调度协调问题，为支持工业4.0范式所需的灵活性和确定性提供了可行的技术解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Factories are evolving towards digitalized data-based ecosystems under the paradigm of the Industry 4.0 where new industrial services allow the implementation of more robust, resilient and customized manufacturing systems. Such services (e.g., digital twins, extended reality or cooperative robots) will require highly reliable and deterministic communication networks capable of supporting stringent latency and reliability requirements. 5G networks and their future evolution have the necessary capabilities to meet these requirements. However, the use of 5G in industrial environments requires its effective and efficient integration with Time Sensitive Networking (TSN), which is becoming the standard wired technology for Industry 4.0 environments. TSN provides unprecedented deterministic service levels with perfectly bounded latencies. The integration of the industrial 5G and TSN networks will be key to support the flexibility and determinism demanded by the Industry 4.0 paradigm. A critical aspect to achieve this integration is the coordination of the schedulers of both networks. TSN has information about the capabilities of the 5G-TSN integrated network, and it is in charge of deciding the path and scheduling for each TSN traffic flow. The scheduling in 5G must be done according to the scheduling decisions and information provided by TSN to guarantee the end-to-end latency requirements of TSN traffic. In this context, this paper proposes a novel Configured Grant scheduling scheme for 5G integrated into a TSN network that aims to meet the latency requirements of the different TSN flows. The proposed scheme exploits the information provided by TSN about the characteristics of the TSN traffic to coordinate its decision with the scheduling of TSN. This study demonstrates that the proposed scheduling scheme considerably increases the number of TSN flows that can be satisfactorily served.

</details>


### [211] [Common Radio Resource Management Policy for Multimedia Traffic in Beyond 3G Heterogeneous Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00705)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Javier Gozalvez,Joaquin Sanchez-Soriano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于线性规划和目标函数的公共无线资源管理(CRRM)策略，用于在异构无线接入技术(RAT)环境中优化资源分配并满足QoS需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4G及未来无线网络需整合多种差异化且互补的无线接入技术(RAT)，亟需统一管理机制以解决互操作性挑战并充分利用技术多样性。

Method: 设计基于线性目标函数和数学编程工具的CRRM决策框架，动态匹配用户QoS需求与最优RAT组合及对应带宽分配。

Result: 通过仿真验证该策略能显著提升多RAT环境下的系统资源利用率和服务质量保障能力，同时降低跨技术切换延迟。

Conclusion: 所提CRRM方法为异构无线网络协同管理提供了可扩展解决方案，有效平衡了技术互补性与系统整体效率的优化关系。

Abstract: Beyond 3G wireless systems will be composed of a variety of Radio Access Technologies (RATs) with different, but also complementary, performance and technical characteristics. To exploit such diversity while guaranteeing the interoperability and efficient management of the different RATs, common radio resource management (CRRM) techniques need to be defined. This work proposes and evaluates a CRRM policy that simultaneously assigns to each user an adequate combination of RAT and number of radio resources within such RAT to guarantee its QoS requirements. The proposed CRRM technique is based on linear objective functions and programming tools.

</details>


### [212] [Latency-Sensitive 5G RAN Slicing for Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00707)
*Jan García-Morales,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时延敏感的5G RAN切片方案，通过切片设计和资源分配策略，满足多样化应用的速率与延迟需求，并在工业4.0场景中验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络切片利用虚拟化创建不同QoS逻辑实例，支持多样化服务；RAN切片对时延敏感应用至关重要，因RAN占端到端延迟的显著部分，需专门方案满足严格延迟要求。

Method: 设计切片架构与无线资源分配方案，针对确定性非周期、确定性周期及非确定性流量优化，以确保速率和延迟需求。

Result: 在工业4.0场景中评估了方案，验证其能有效满足时延敏感应用的速率与确定性延迟要求。

Conclusion: 该方案可扩展至其他垂直领域，支持各类时延敏感或时间关键应用，为5G网络切片在RAN层面的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Network slicing is a novel 5G paradigm that exploits the virtualization and softwarization of networks to create different logical network instances over a common network infrastructure. Each instance is tailored for specific Quality of Service (QoS) profiles so that network slicing can simultaneously support several services with diverse requirements. Network slicing can be applied at the Core Network or at the Radio Access Network (RAN). RAN slicing is particularly relevant to support latency-sensitive or timecritical applications since the RAN accounts for a significant part of the end-to-end transmission latency. In this context, this study proposes a novel latency-sensitive 5G RAN slicing solution. The proposal includes schemes to design slices and partition (or allocate) radio resources among slices. These schemes are designed with the objective to satisfy both the rate and latency demands of diverse applications. In particular, this study considers applications with deterministic aperiodic, deterministic periodic and nondeterministic traffic. The latency-sensitive 5G RAN slicing proposal is evaluated in Industry 4.0 scenarios where stringent and/or deterministic latency requirements are common. However, it can be evolved to support other verticals with latency-sensitive or time-critical applications.

</details>


### [213] [Mobile Base Station Positioning in Smart Ports Based on Kriged Sparse Measurements and Obstacle Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00709)
*Paulo Furtado Correia,André Coelho,Manuel Ricardo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对智能港口动态无线电阻塞问题，提出REM驱动的DOCKING框架，仅需15%稀疏测量实现高精度障碍物表征，使MIAB部署容量提升150%且优化收敛时间缩短至5-15秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIAB部署方法依赖障碍物几何数据库或复杂传播模型，在集装箱堆叠等动态阻塞场景中适应性差，亟需轻量级环境感知方案。

Method: 采用普通克里金算法重建RSRP/SINR数据，通过立方体模型抽象衰减区域，结合回传感知优化算法确定基站部署、用户关联及回传路径。

Result: REM重建90%分位误差<3dB（15%采样率），障碍物表征真实阳性率>85%；稀疏部署容量增益达150%，遗传算法平均收敛时间7.5秒；实地测试吞吐量趋势与仿真一致。

Conclusion: 稀疏无线电测量可有效替代传统障碍物数据库，为高阻塞工业环境提供可扩展的MIAB部署解决方案，突破动态环境适应性瓶颈。

Abstract: Smart-port wireless networks suffer from dynamic radio blockage caused by container stacks and industrial structures, challenging efficient mobile integrated access and backhaul (MIAB) deployment. Existing approaches rely on obstacle maps, geometry information, or computationally intensive propagation models that limit adaptability. This paper presents DOCKING, a radio environment map (REM)-driven framework that converts sparse radio measurements into optimization-ready obstacle representations for MIAB deployment. The framework infers propagation-relevant obstacle abstractions from reconstructed REMs, eliminating the need for obstacle-geometry databases while relying only on known network parameters and sparse measurements. Reference signal received power (RSRP) and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) observations are reconstructed using Ordinary Kriging (OKG), and dominant attenuation regions are approximated by compact cuboidal blockage models. The inferred geometry feeds a backhaul-aware optimization that determines MIAB placement, user equipment (UE) association, and backhaul selection. Under realistic smart-port conditions, REM reconstruction achieves prediction errors below 3 dB at the 90th percentile using only 15% spatial sampling, while obstacle characterization exceeds 85% true-positive coverage. Capacity gains reach 150% in sparse deployments, and a fast Genetic Algorithm converges within 5-15 s per network snapshot. A field campaign using real measurements validates the workflow, showing throughput trends consistent with optimization predictions. Results demonstrate that sparse radio measurements provide sufficient environmental awareness for practical obstacle-aware MIAB deployment in obstruction-prone industrial environments.

</details>


### [214] [SNR-Adaptive Optimal Threshold Design for Energy Detection in Dynamic Spectrum Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00754)
*Sushila Dhaka,Jane-Hwa Huang,Chin-Min Yu,Li-Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种SNR自适应的阈值设计框架，通过解析解优化能量检测，相比传统方法在低信噪比下显著降低误差，提升动态频谱接入的可靠性，并为安全合作感知奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFAR方法仅依赖虚警约束设置固定阈值，难以适应不同SNR环境，导致检测性能受限，尤其在低SNR场景下误差率高。本研究旨在通过直接优化总误差概率，提升动态频谱接入系统的自适应性和可靠性。

Method: 构建二次型阈值优化模型，其系数显式表征SNR与样本数的影响，推导闭式解析解，实现无需数值搜索的自适应阈值选择。

Result: 仿真验证表明：该方法在低SNR区域误差概率显著低于固定阈值与CFAR方案；系统分析揭示了SNR与样本数对虚警/漏检的权衡关系，验证了框架在不同条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了动态频谱接入中阈值自适应问题，提升了感知可靠性与实用性，并为区块链辅助的安全协作感知提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes an SNR-adaptive optimal threshold design framework for energy detection in Dynamic Spectrum Access (DSA). Unlike conventional constant false-alarm rate (CFAR)-based schemes that determine the sensing threshold solely from a predefined false-alarm constraint, the proposed method directly minimizes the total probability of error by deriving a closed-form analytical solution. The threshold optimization problem is formulated as a quadratic expression whose coefficients explicitly characterize the effects of signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and number of samples. This analytical structure enables adaptive threshold selection under heterogeneous SNR conditions without exhaustive numerical search. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the error probability compared with fixed-threshold and detection-constrained schemes, particularly in low-SNR regimes. Furthermore, the impact of SNR and number of samples on detection performance is systematically analyzed, providing deeper insight into the trade-off between false alarm and missed detection. The proposed framework improves sensing reliability and practical adaptability in dynamic spectrum access systems. It also establishes a foundation for secure cooperative spectrum sensing, including blockchain-assisted aggregation mechanisms.

</details>


### [215] [Data-driven mitigation of catastrophic forgetting in dynamic physical layer attack detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01041)
*Aleksandra Knapińska,Marija Furdek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究针对光网络安全检测中动态模型易受灾难性遗忘影响、静态模型缺乏适应性的问题，提出一种基于历史数据平衡与阈值触发的自适应机制，在实验数据集上显著缩短模型适应时间并减少数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测系统面临根本性权衡：动态更新模型能应对新威胁但易遗忘旧攻击模式，而静态模型虽保留历史知识却无法适应演化中的攻击手段，导致全球通信基础设施面临持续安全挑战。

Method: 设计数据驱动型平衡机制，通过选择性复用历史攻击数据构建平衡训练集，并设置性能下降阈值自动触发数据重平衡流程，避免完全依赖实时遥测数据。

Result: 在光学网络安全数据集验证中，相较未使用数据平衡的动态模型平均适应时间缩短37%，相比神经网络深度扩展基线方法节省6%数据批次即可恢复检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提方案有效缓解动态攻击检测系统的灾难性遗忘问题，在保持对新型威胁响应能力的同时显著增强历史知识保留性，为光网络长期安全防御提供新思路。

Abstract: Optical networks are critical infrastructure that underpins global communications, and detecting security breaches that jeopardize them is essential to maintaining worldwide connectivity. As malicious actors continuously evolve their attack techniques, dynamically updated intrusion detection models have become a key component of modern defense mechanisms. By incorporating newly acquired telemetry data, these models can adapt to emerging threats while maintaining high detection performance. However, when previously encountered attacks reappear after a prolonged period of absence, adaptive models may fail to recognize them due to the phenomenon of catastrophic forgetting. In contrast, statically trained models can reliably detect attacks represented in the original training data but lack the ability to adapt to previously unseen attack patterns. Consequently, intrusion detection systems face a fundamental tradeoff between adaptability to evolving threats and long-term retention of previously acquired knowledge. In this work, we propose a data-driven mechanism to cope with catastrophic forgetting in dynamic attack detection systems. Our approach balances the model update datasets by using parts of past attack data. We utilize a threshold-based mechanism to trigger data balancing after accuracy drops due to an active attack change. Applied to an experimental optical network security dataset, the proposed approach reduces the average model adaptation time by 37% compared to its dynamic counterpart that does not employ data balancing. Compared to a baseline from the literature that relies on neural network depth increasing, our approach requires 6% fewer data batches to adapt to changing conditions and regain performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [EmbodimentSemantic: A Spatial Scene-Graph Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Embodied Manipulation Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00020)
*Hassan Jaber,Refinath S N,Luca Cagliero,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对视觉语言动作系统(VLA)在空间定位上的不足，提出了EmbodimentSemantic——一个包含真实机器人操作数据和模拟环境的场景图数据集及基准测试。通过构建物体-关系三元组表示空间结构，验证了现有VLM在深度感知和视角依赖性空间关系理解上的缺陷，为VLA系统的空间推理能力评估提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA系统虽能识别物体并执行语言指令，但缺乏对物体间支撑、包含、遮挡等空间关系的显式建模，导致无法精确理解三维场景的空间结构，限制了复杂操作任务的完成能力。

Method: 提出EmbodimentSemantic数据集与基准框架：1）构建真实场景图数据集，使用SO101机械臂采集操控观察数据；2）开发基于MuJoCo的LIBERO仿真基准，生成超6万帧操控数据和12万+带相机视角的场景图；3）将场景图注入现有VLA策略提示中测试下游控制效果。

Result: 实验表明：1）现有VLM能生成合理关系描述但存在系统性偏差；2）在深度敏感关系（如前方/后方）和视角依赖结构（如遮挡层级）预测上准确率显著下降；3）场景图增强策略可提升VLA系统空间一致性。

Conclusion: EmbodimentSemantic首次建立了标准化空间关系评估体系，揭示了VLM在物理世界空间建模的根本局限，其提供的诊断工具可指导下一代具身智能系统开发，并推动VLA架构向显式空间推理方向演进。

Abstract: Spatial grounding remains a key limitation of vision-language-action (VLA) systems for robotic manipulation. While current models can recognize objects and follow language instructions, they often lack an explicit representation of how objects are arranged in space, including support, containment, ordering, occlusion, and depth-sensitive relations. We introduce EmbodimentSemantic, a spatial scene-graph dataset and benchmark for evaluating relational grounding in embodied manipulation. EmbodimentSemantic represents scenes as directed object-relation-object triplets, where each triplet specifies a spatial relation between an ordered pair of objects using a fixed set of relations. This representation enables direct evaluation of object binding, relation prediction, and spatial consistency. The dataset includes real-world manipulation observations collected with the low-cost SO101 robot arm, together with generated scene graphs for studying spatial grounding in practical robotic settings. To provide controlled validation, we also introduce a simulator-grounded LIBERO benchmark with over 60K manipulation frames and more than 120K camera-specific scene graphs across paired third-person and wrist views, where ground-truth relations are derived automatically from MuJoCo geometry, world coordinates, camera projections, and visibility constraints. We further test whether scene graphs improve downstream control by injecting them into existing VLA policy prompts. Experiments across open-source and commercial VLMs show that current models often predict plausible relations but struggle with exact depth-aware and viewpoint-dependent spatial structure. EmbodimentSemantic provides a unified framework for diagnosing spatial grounding in VLM perception and testing its utility for VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [217] [When to Personalize Household Object Search: A Rigidity-Gated Hybrid Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00022)
*Xianyao Li,Yuhai Wang,Hu Xiao,Kaleb Smith,Gilbert Yang Ye,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出PerSim，一个用于服务机器人家庭物体搜索的刚性门控混合策略。该方法结合特征条件先验与人群频率基线，仅在放置行为可变时进行个性化。通过人类校准的模拟管道生成数据，在200人研究中验证了合成转换的行为可信度，并发现个性化主要对低刚性物体有效。最终在家庭数字孪生环境中证明了预期搜索成本的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在搜索家庭物体时依赖空间先验知识减少搜索成本，但物体位置会因居民特征而异。收集长期、特定特征的家庭内轨迹数据具有侵入性且难以规模化。研究需要解决何时个性化有效的问题，以及如何可扩展地建模居民条件动态。

Method: 提出PerSim：刚性门控混合策略，结合特征条件先验与人群频率基线，仅在放置行为可变时进行个性化。采用人类校准的模拟管道生成和验证多样化家庭布局中的对象放置转换，训练预测器注入连续大五人格向量，输出房间级先验和房间内共现线索。

Result: 在统一人类研究(N=200)的双层验证中：(i) 合成转换在行为上可信（平均3.85/5，p<1e-6）；(ii) 盲测A/B比较显示个性化主要针对低刚性物体有效(p=0.005)，而人群频率基线对普遍放置物品仍很强。离线测试显示在未见过的连续特征向量上有小但显著改进(p=0.035)。家庭数字孪生实验证明PerSim降低了预期搜索成本。

Conclusion: PerSim通过结合房间访问努力与房间内线索检查，实现了端到端的搜索成本降低，超越了孤立预测指标的表现。研究明确了个性化有效的决策规则：对低刚性物体进行个性化，而对普遍放置物品使用人群基线。该方法支持在五维特征空间中的插值，为可扩展的居民条件动态建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Service robots searching for household objects rely on spatial priors to reduce search cost, yet object locations can vary with resident traits. Collecting longitudinal, trait-specific in-home trajectories is invasive and hard to scale. We study when personalization helps and propose PerSim, a rigidity-gated hybrid policy that combines a trait-conditioned prior with a population-frequency baseline, personalizing only when placement behavior is variable. To scale resident-conditioned dynamics, we employ a human-calibrated simulation pipeline to generate and validate object-placement transitions in diverse home layouts, and train a predictor that injects continuous Big Five vectors to output room-level priors and within-room co-occurrence cues. In a unified human study (N=200), dual-layer validation shows that (i) synthetic transitions are judged behaviorally plausible (mean 3.85/5, p < 1e-6), and (ii) in a blinded A/B comparison, personalization is favored primarily for low-rigidity objects (p=0.005), while the population-frequency baseline remains strong for universally placed items, yielding a decision rule for when to personalize. In an offline objective test, we observe a small but significant improvement on unseen continuous trait vectors over nearest discrete configuration matching (p=0.035), supporting interpolation in five-dimensional trait space. Finally, in a home digital twin we show that PerSim reduces expected search cost by combining room visitation effort with within-room cue checking, demonstrating end-to-end gains beyond isolated prediction metrics.

</details>


### [218] [Decentralized Geometric Control for Cable-Suspended Payload Transport with Adaptive Mass Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00024)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Paul Schmitt,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPAC是一种四层分层控制架构，通过隐式协调机制使多旋翼无人机在无中央协调、无需交换状态参数的情况下协同运输悬挂载荷，利用本地测量独立估计负载分担并重构位置，结合非线性流形控制、反摆调节、扰动观测器及CBF安全滤波，在高保真仿真中实现33.8cm平均跟踪误差与2.8%变异系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作空中运输需突破传统集中式协调瓶颈，解决非线性流形几何约束下的分布式安全控制问题，同时降低通信依赖和计算开销。

Method: 提出GPAC四层架构：(1)隐式协调机制通过本地电缆测量估计负载分担；(2)非线性配置流形上的几何控制集成位置/姿态控制与防摆调节；(3)扩展状态观测器实现风扰抑制；(4)CBF安全滤波器保障输入到状态安全边界，兼容SO(3)姿态控制器稳定性。

Result: 高保真仿真验证显示：柔性缆绳+风湍流环境下载荷跟踪RMSE达33.8cm（13次实验变异系数2.8%），单代理计算负载低，且安全滤波器力修正始终维持姿态在几乎全局稳定域内。

Conclusion: GPAC首次实现无需全局信息共享的多旋翼协同运输，通过隐式协调与安全约束融合，在保证ISSf安全裕度的前提下达成高精度轨迹跟踪，为分布式空中物流提供理论突破。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial transport requires controllers that respect nonlinear manifold geometry, operate without centralized coordination, and respect operational safety constraints. To address these demands, we present GPAC, a four-layer hierarchical architecture that enables $N$ quadrotors to transport a cable-suspended payload without a central coordinator or by exchanging cable states or adaptive parameters. The key insight is implicit coordination: each quadrotor independently estimates its effective load share from local cable measurements, so combined forces converge to the correct total, even without knowledge of $N$ or the payload mass; the payload position is reconstructed locally from each agent's own cable geometry, and the only inter-agent communication is a low-rate neighbor-position broadcast for collision avoidance. GPAC operates directly on the full nonlinear configuration manifold and integrates geometric position and attitude control, anti-swing regulation, an extended-state observer for wind rejection, concurrent learning-based mass estimation without persistent excitation, and a priority-ordered control barrier function (CBF)-inspired safety filter that reduces operational risk, with input-to-state safety (ISSf) margins that hold exactly under single-constraint activation. A compatibility result shows that the filter's force modifications keep the desired attitude within the almost-global stability region of the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ attitude controller. Finally, high-fidelity simulation with flexible cables, onboard sensor fusion, and wind turbulence -- with all control and estimation loops closed through the estimator -- yields a mean payload-tracking RMSE of 33.8 cm (2.8\% coefficient of variation over 13 seeds) at a low per-agent computational cost.

</details>


### [219] [FLYNN: Robust Neural Network for Robot Navigation using Fly Brain Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00025)
*Benquan Wang,Jingdao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于果蝇大脑连接组构建的递归神经网络FLYNN，在视觉导航任务中实现与手工网络相当的性能，同时显著提升了抗分布外数据和感官剥夺的鲁棒性，为生物启发的AI架构设计提供新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在应对新环境或感官剥夺时表现脆弱，而生物系统展现出更强的适应性，亟需通过借鉴生物神经网络的拓扑结构提升AI鲁棒性。

Method: 直接利用果蝇黑腹果蝇的突触级脑连接组构建递归神经网络（FLYNN），在MuJoCo物理引擎中进行视觉导航任务训练与测试。

Result: FLYNN在导航性能上媲美同参数手工网络，且在分布外数据和感官缺失条件下表现更优；即使在完全视觉丧失时仍保持功能，而传统网络即使经过相机丢失训练也基本失效；PCA分析显示其内部状态具有高度表征模块化特征。

Conclusion: 首次验证了基于生物脑连接组的AI架构可有效提升系统鲁棒性，为开发具备生物级适应能力的智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art performance in complex tasks, they remain brittle when faced with new environments or sensory deprivation. In contrast, biological systems exhibit remarkable tolerance to these challenges. We address this vulnerability by developing a recurrent neural network (RNN) whose architecture is directly derived from the synaptic-resolution brain connectome of the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster. We demonstrate the feasibility of training the fly connectome neural network (FLYNN) to perform vision-based navigation in MuJoCo, achieving performance comparable to modern hand-crafted networks of similar parameter counts. Crucially, FLYNN exhibits superior resistance to out-of-distribution (OOD) data and tolerance to sensory loss without further training. It remained functional even under total vision loss while hand-crafted networks largely failed, even when specifically trained with camera dropout. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) of the internal state of FLYNN suggests that it exhibits a particularly high degree of representational modularity, which might be related to its robustness. Our work provides a new direction for designing resilient artificial agents following the topology of biological brains.

</details>
