### [256] [DART-VLN: Test-Time Memory Decay and Anti-Loop Regularization for Discrete Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01043)
*Shaoheng Zhang,Zhichen Li,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-VLN improves discrete VLN through test-time memory decay and anti-loop penalties, achieving better reliability/efficiency without model retraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address partial observability challenges in memory-based VLN where frozen backbones suffer from stale historical evidence during memory retrieval and inefficient local backtracking during action selection.

Method: DART-VLN combines Test-Time Memory Decay (read-side reweighting to suppress stale/redundant memory) and Anti-Loop Regularization (next-hop penalty to prevent immediate reversals), both implemented without new parameters or backbone modifications.

Result: Experiments on R2R/REVERIE demonstrate: (1) Decay-only provides consistent read-side improvements; (2) Combined approach achieves optimal trade-off with shorter paths, lower runtime, and better navigation; (3) Anti-loop regularization reduces backtracking behavior while maintaining performance under frozen models.

Conclusion: Testing-time control methods without retraining can significantly improve reliability and efficiency of memory-based discrete VLN systems.

Abstract: Memory-based discrete vision-language navigation (VLN) agents must act under partial observability, yet even strong frozen backbones remain vulnerable at test time. Two common failure modes are stale historical evidence at memory readout and inefficient local backtracking during action selection. We present DART-VLN, a training-free test-time control framework for discrete VLN. DART-VLN combines Test-Time Memory Decay, a read-side memory reweighting rule that suppresses stale and redundant evidence without rewriting stored content, with Anti-Loop Regularization, a lightweight next-hop penalty that discourages immediate reversals during action selection. The framework introduces no new learnable parameters and leaves the learned backbone unchanged. Experiments on R2R and REVERIE show a consistent pattern: decay-only provides stable read-side gains, while decay+anti-loop achieves the best overall quality-efficiency trade-off, yielding shorter trajectories, lower runtime, and improved navigation performance in key settings. Behavioral analysis further confirms that anti-loop regularization reduces local backtracking and improves path efficiency under frozen backbones. Overall, the results show that modest test-time control can make memory-based discrete VLN more reliable and efficient without retraining.

</details>


### [257] [Robots Ask the Way: Communication-Enabled Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01044)
*Valentino Sacco,Luca Scofano,Indro Spinelli,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Assistive autonomous robots operating in multi-agent environments require efficient strategies to locate specific individuals among multiple residents. Current social navigation methods focus on reactive collision avoidance and trajectory adaptation, but lack mechanisms to proactively gather information through human-robot communication.
  We introduce Communication-enabled Social Navigation (CommNav). In this novel task, robotic agents actively seek assistance from residents to locate target individuals by requesting information about recent sightings, locations, and movements.
  To evaluate CommNav, we extend Habitat 3.0 to create Habitat 3.0c, a communication-enabled variant supporting multi-human environments with information exchange protocols. Adding our communication module (COMM) to a state-of-the-art social navigation model yields a 10 percentage-point improvement in Episode Success. We further investigate the transition from structured data to natural language by evaluating models trained on LLM-generated instructions and on colloquial instructions collected from a human study.
  Our experiments reveal that: (i) explicit human-robot communication substantially enhances multi-person navigation performance; (ii) pre-training COMM on a communication pretext task effectively addresses the challenge of occasional interaction signals; and (iii) the navigation policy is highly robust to natural, colloquial human language, achieving an episode success statistically similar to the model using perfect structured data.

</details>


### [258] [AutoSpeed: Annotation-Free Stage-Adaptive Motion Speed Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01051)
*Qingda Hu,Ziheng Qiu,Jieru Zhao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AutoSpeed框架，通过无标注阶段自适应速度调节提升视觉运动策略效率，利用频域DCT实现平滑速度缩放，显著缩短任务时间并提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IL-based策略采用固定执行速度和预测视界，难以适应操作任务中不同阶段的动态难度变化，导致灵活性不足和整体吞吐量受限。

Method: 设计模型无关框架：1) 将多速度轨迹作为优化候选；2) 构建预测误差与视界长度的复合成本函数；3) 通过DCT在频域实现非整数倍速度调制，确保运动连续性且无需额外标注。

Result: 实验证明：任务执行时间显著降低，成功率同步提升，且推断速度分布与任务阶段存在强相关性，验证了阶段自适应机制的有效性。

Conclusion: AutoSpeed通过无监督速度调制突破传统固定视界限制，为复杂操作任务提供可扩展的时序优化方案，在机器人控制领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Different stages of manipulation tasks exhibit varying levels of difficulty, suggesting stage-dependent motion speeds and temporal prediction horizons. However, existing IL-based visuomotor policies typically imitate the execution speed of expert demonstrations and operate with a fixed temporal prediction horizon, limiting flexibility and overall task throughput. In this paper, we introduce AutoSpeed, a model-agnostic learning framework that enables existing visuomotor policies to predict trajectories with stage-adaptive motion speeds, without requiring speed or stage annotations. We treat future trajectories at different speeds as candidate optimization targets, evaluate each candidate using a composite cost that trades off prediction error against prediction horizon, and optimize the policy toward the minimum-cost candidate. With a fixed-length action sequence, speed modulation adjusts the effective temporal prediction horizon: simple stages are executed faster with a longer prediction horizon, whereas complex stages are executed more slowly with a shorter prediction horizon. Specifically, we implement speed modulation in the frequency domain via the discrete cosine transform (DCT), which enables smooth, non-integer speed scaling and thus preserves motion continuity. Extensive evaluations show that AutoSpeed substantially reduces task execution time while also improving success rates. Under the AutoSpeed framework, the inferred motion speeds exhibit a strong correspondence with task stages.

</details>


### [259] [RoboWorld: Fast and Reliable Neural Simulators for Generalist Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01060)
*Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,JaeHyeok Doo,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWorld是一个自动化评估流水线，结合快速自回归视频世界模型和任务进度感知的视觉语言模型评分，用于可靠评估通用机器人策略，在长时序自回归滚动中实现与真实世界机器人评估的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型是评估通用机器人策略的可扩展替代方案，能够绕过物理约束和工程负担，但目前仍面临世界模型错误导致生成的滚动不可靠以及推理速度慢限制大规模吞吐量的挑战。

Method: 提出RoboWorld评估流水线，包含两个核心组件：1) 快速自回归视频世界模型；2) 任务进度感知的视觉语言模型评分。特别提出Step Forcing技术，结合锚定上下文和单步自转发上下文，减少训练-测试不匹配的同时保持动作-观察动态。

Result: RoboWorld与真实世界机器人评估在任务和环境上高度对齐，实现Pearson相关系数r = 0.989和Spearman相关系数ρ = 0.970，显示出卓越的评估准确性。

Conclusion: RoboWorld成功解决了视频世界模型评估的挑战，通过Step Forcing技术实现可靠的长时序自回归滚动，为机器人策略评估提供了高效、准确的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Video world models are emerging as a scalable alternative for evaluating generalist robot policies, bypassing the physical constraints and engineering burdens of real-world deployment. However, evaluating policies with video world models remains challenging, as world-model errors can make generated rollouts unreliable and slow inference limits large-scale throughput. We introduce RoboWorld, an automated evaluation pipeline that pairs a fast autoregressive video world model with a task-progress-aware vision-language model scoring. To enable reliable long-horizon autoregressive world-model rollouts, we propose Step Forcing, which combines anchored and one-step self-forwarded contexts to reduce train--test mismatch while preserving action--observation dynamics. Together, these components enable RoboWorld to align strongly with real-world robot evaluation across tasks and environments, achieving Pearson's r = 0.989 and Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.970.

</details>


### [260] [Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01067)
*Chi Zhang,Penglin Cai,Ziheng Xi,Haoqi Yuan,Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Sipeng Zheng,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Tac大规模触觉-动作数据集（160小时视频/300+任务/13.5万集）及可迁移触觉预训练（TTP）框架，通过统一触觉与动作空间实现人类到机器人的知识迁移，结合触觉专家模型建模接触动力学，显著提升机器人精细操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉数据集规模小且接触覆盖有限，基于触觉的VLA模型因缺乏动态感知后训练导致下游任务性能瓶颈。需突破硬件/数据采集限制，解决人类与机器人触觉交互差异问题。

Method: 1) 构建H-Tac：160小时第一视角人类视频，覆盖300+任务；2) 提出TTP框架：统一触觉/动作空间进行跨阶段迁移学习，利用触觉专家模型预测未来触觉信号以显式建模接触动力学。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中，模型展现优异的泛化能力与精细操作性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 首次实现大规模触觉预训练的人类到机器人迁移范式，为具身智能系统提供可扩展的接触感知解决方案，突破传统视觉主导的操作局限。

Abstract: As an essential modality for dexterous and contact-rich tasks, tactile sensing provides precise force feedback that cannot be reliably inferred from vision. However, limited by hardware and data collection systems, existing datasets with tactility remain small in scale and narrow in contact coverage. Meanwhile, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with tactile modality are constrained on dynamics-agnostic post-training, which limits the performance ceiling on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset with 160-hour egocentric human videos containing more than 300 tasks and 135k episodes. Building upon this, we propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a system of tactile-based pre-training on human data for fine-grained robotic tasks. To bridge the gap between humans and robots, we use unified tactile and action spaces throughout the pre-training and post-training phases, preserving prior knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By leveraging a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, our framework explicitly models the contact dynamics and precise physical interactions. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance, exhibiting robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities. TTP paves the way for scalable tactile pre-training via human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [261] [Where Am I? Semantic Map Grounding via Vision-Language Models for Multi-Modal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01079)
*Suraj Borate,Aarav Shah,Madhu Vadali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉-语言模型的室内机器人定位新方法，将定位问题重构为语义推理任务。通过微调Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型，结合摄像头图像、LiDAR扫描和语义地图，直接预测机器人位姿坐标。在自定义数据集上实现了98.23%位置准确率和96.75%完整位姿准确率，并在多种挑战场景下表现出良好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GPS拒绝室内环境中的机器人定位问题。受人类利用物体级别线索和标注地图进行定位的启发，研究者探索视觉-语言模型是否能通过语义推理实现机器人定位，而非传统的几何估计方法。

Method: 1. 将机器人定位重构为语义推理任务
2. 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B视觉-语言模型作为基础架构
3. 通过LoRA进行微调，添加轻量级回归头直接预测连续位姿坐标(x, y, theta)
4. 融合多模态输入：前置摄像头图像、极坐标LiDAR扫描、俯视语义网格地图
5. 采用位置-方向复合损失函数和课程学习策略
6. 在自定义Gazebo数据集上训练，包含120,112个样本和527个场景

Result: 1. 在18,017个样本的分布内测试集上取得优异性能：位置准确率98.23%、方向准确率98.00%、完整位姿准确率96.75%
2. 平均位置误差0.11m，平均方向误差5.7度，推理速度0.62s/样本
3. 在7个未见过的物体类别上位置准确率仍达90.99%，仅下降7.2个百分点
4. 使用不完整地图时，微调后位置准确率恢复至93.72%
5. 消融实验显示：仅用摄像头和地图时位置准确率为95.06%；无摄像头物体可见时LiDAR维持92.33%准确率

Conclusion: 该方法证明了基于语义推理的机器人定位的可行性，展现出显著的语义空间推理能力而非外观记忆。系统在不同传感器配置下表现出良好的鲁棒性和适应性，特别是LiDAR在摄像头语义不可用时提供可靠的备用定位信号，适用于遮挡或稀疏布局场景。

Abstract: We address robot localization in GPS-denied indoor environments by reframing it as a semantic reasoning task rather than a geometric estimation problem. Motivated by how humans localize using object-level cues and labeled maps, we ask whether a vision-language model, given a front camera image, a polar LiDAR scan, and a top-down semantic grid map, can infer the robot pose. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-7B with LoRA and attach a lightweight regression head that predicts continuous pose coordinates (x, y, theta) directly from the final hidden state, bypassing text generation. Training uses a composite position-and-direction loss with curriculum learning on a custom Gazebo dataset of 120,112 samples and 527 scenes. On the in-distribution test set of 18,017 samples, the model achieves 98.23 percent position accuracy, 98.00 percent direction accuracy, 96.75 percent full pose accuracy, a mean position error of 0.11 m, and a mean orientation error of 5.7 degrees at 0.62 s per sample. Position accuracy drops by only 7.2 percentage points on seven unseen object categories, reaching 90.99 percent, supporting semantic spatial reasoning rather than appearance memorization. With incomplete maps, fine-tuning recovers performance to 93.72 percent position accuracy, showing adaptability to stale or partial map information. Two ablations highlight cross-modal complementarity. Without LiDAR, using only camera and map inputs, position accuracy remains 95.06 percent, only 3.2 percentage points below the full system. However, when the camera sees no visible objects in a wall-facing view, LiDAR sustains 92.33 percent position accuracy, compared with 70.74 percent when neither LiDAR nor visible objects are available. This shows that LiDAR becomes the primary localization signal when camera semantics are unavailable and provides a reliable fallback under occlusion or sparse layouts.

</details>


### [262] [ROSA: A Robotics Foundation Model Serving System for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01088)
*Wenqi Jiang,Jason Clemons,Rowland O'Flaherty,Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Shuran Song,Yashraj Narang,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSA是一个面向机器人工厂的RFM服务系统，通过共享GPU池、机器人感知抽象和工厂目标驱动调度，将生产力提升12.06倍，突破了传统单机器人单模型假设的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFM服务系统基于单机器人、单模型假设，将推理视为边缘计算问题，由机器人本地或附近专用GPU处理，目标是最小化单个动作模型延迟，导致GPU利用率低、电池续航短、推理性能受限，无法满足工厂级部署需求。

Method: ROSA基于三个核心原则：1) 共享GPU池服务，使机器人车队通过网络访问服务器级GPU；2) 机器人感知编程抽象，支持多模型流水线、任务级性能要求和故障处理；3) 工厂目标驱动调度，以最大化SLO合格工厂生产力为优化目标而非单个请求延迟。系统基于Ray Serve实现分布式编排，支持vLLM、PyTorch和JAX后端。

Result: 在真实机器人和合成大规模工作负载上的评估显示，ROSA相比传统专用服务系统，将工厂生产力最高提升12.06倍，同时改善了推理性能、电池续航和GPU利用率。

Conclusion: ROSA通过系统性创新突破了现有RFM服务架构的局限，为工厂级多机器人部署提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，验证了共享资源池和工厂级优化目标的实际价值。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models (RFMs) are making general-purpose robots increasingly practical for factory deployments. While RFM serving systems are central to this vision, existing systems are largely shaped by a single-robot, single-model assumption: inference is treated as an edge-computing problem handled by an on-robot or dedicated nearby GPU, and the serving objective is to minimize the latency of a single action model. In this paper, we propose ROSA, an RFM serving system for robot factories designed around three key principles. First, ROSA adopts shared GPU-pool serving, allowing a fleet of robots to access powerful server-class GPUs over the network in order to improve inference performance, battery duration, and GPU utilization. Second, ROSA provides a robotics-aware programming abstraction and system design that supports multi-model pipelines, per-task performance requirements, and failure handling. Third, ROSA uses factory-objective-driven scheduling to maximize SLO-qualified factory productivity rather than minimizing individual request latency. We implement ROSA on top of Ray Serve for distributed orchestration, with vLLM, PyTorch, and JAX as model-serving backends, and evaluate it on both real robots and synthetic large-scale workloads. The results show that ROSA improves factory productivity by up to 12.06x over conventional dedicated serving systems.

</details>


### [263] [Technical Report: Asynchronous Distributed Trajectory Estimation of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01106)
*Adam Pooley,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种异步块坐标下降算法解决分布式轨迹估计中的通信与计算异步问题，显著减少通信量并提升估计精度，在模拟与真实机器人实验中验证了其鲁棒性和优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式轨迹估计方法普遍未考虑多智能体间通信与计算的异步性，导致实际应用效率低下或准确性不足。

Method: 设计异步块坐标下降算法，通过求解滑动窗口下的最大后验估计近似问题，引入误差可忽略的近似模型以减少智能体间的通信需求。

Result: 通信量减少达96.9%，误差较当前最优方法降低64%；理论证明迭代过程呈指数级收敛，实验显示该算法能有效应对跨度三个数量级的通信延迟。

Conclusion: 所提算法在保证估计精度的同时大幅优化了分布式系统的通信效率，为异步环境下的机器人状态估计提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed trajectory estimation arises in many applications across robotics, but existing implementations typically do not consider asynchrony in agents' communications and computations. Therefore, we propose an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm for distributed trajectory estimation. We consider a team of agents that observes a team of robots and estimates their states over a sliding window. The agents solve an approximation of the maximum a posteriori estimation problem, which we derive. We show this approximation introduces negligible errors and eliminates up to 96.9% of communications among agents. Next, we prove that agents' iterates converge exponentially fast to the optimal estimate of the robots' states. Simulations show that this approach has up to 64% less error than a comparable state-of-the-art algorithm. Experiments on mobile robots show the robustness of this approach to delays whose lengths span three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [264] [FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01111)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: The paper introduces Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework enabling robots to autonomously learn from failures during test-time via Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation (steering policies away from unsuccessful behaviors) and action perturbations for exploration. FAR integrates successful recoveries into continuous training loops, achieving 17.6% higher success rates in simulation and 11.7% in real-world tasks compared to diffusion policies, while significantly improving data efficiency under reset/timestep constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robots face unavoidable deployment failures; naive retries repeat mistakes and existing recovery methods depend on human intervention, limiting autonomy.

Method: FAR combines: 1) Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation (FCPA) to construct preference data from failures for policy steering, and 2) lightweight action perturbations during retries for local exploration. Successful recovery trajectories are fed back into training for continual policy refinement.

Result: Substantial improvements in success rates (17.6% simulation, 11.7% real-world vs diffusion policy) and enhanced data efficiency in both reset and timestep budgets during continual learning.

Conclusion: FAR enables robust autonomous failure recovery through failure-guided learning and iterative refinement, offering scalable solutions for real-world robotic deployments without human oversight.

Abstract: Robot policies inevitably encounter failures when deployed in real environments. Naive retries often repeat the same mistakes, while many existing recovery methods rely on human intervention. In this paper, we propose Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework that enables robots to learn from previous failures at test time, adapt their behavior accordingly, and eventually complete the task autonomously. FAR combines Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation, which constructs preference learning data from failures to steer the policy away from previously unsuccessful behaviors, with lightweight action perturbations during retries to encourage local exploration. We further incorporate successful recovery trajectories into a training loop for continual policy improvement. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks show that FAR substantially improves success rates and robustness, with average gains of 17.6% over the standard diffusion policy in simulation and 11.7% in the real world. In addition, FAR significantly improves data efficiency under both reset and timestep budgets during continual policy improvement by exploiting informative failure cases.

</details>


### [265] [Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01166)
*Zhiyi Li,Peilin Wu,Xiaoshen Han,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结构化的4D潜在预测模型，通过3D场景编码提升机器人规划的空间一致性和泛化能力，优于现有视频方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预测模型依赖2D序列，缺乏3D几何理解能力，导致空间推理精度和物理一致性问题，限制机器人在复杂任务中的表现。

Method: 设计结构化4D潜在空间预测框架，结合观测数据与文本指令编码场景演化，通过可解码多格式3D表示实现全局建模；联合目标条件逆向动力学模块将预测结果转化为可执行动作。

Result: 实验证明该模型在视觉质量、3D一致性和多视角连贯性上显著超越现有视频规划器，所构建的完整规划管道在复杂操作任务中性能更优，且对新型视觉条件具有强泛化性，已在真实机器人平台验证有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化4D潜在表示突破2D视频模型的局限性，在保持生成质量的同时实现物理一致性建模，为机器人长期规划提供可靠基础，推动通用具身智能发展。

Abstract: Video predictive models are emerging as a powerful paradigm in robotics, offering a promising path toward task generalization, long-horizon planning, and flexible decision-making. However, prevailing approaches often operate on 2D video sequences, inherently lacking the 3D geometric understanding necessary for precise spatial reasoning and physical consistency. We introduce a Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model, which predicts the evolution of a scene's 3D structure in a structured latent space conditioned on observations and textual instructions. Our representation encodes the scene holistically and can be decoded into diverse 3D formats, enabling a more complete and 3D consistent scene understanding. This structured 4D latent predictive model serves as a planner, generating future scenes that are translated into executable actions by a goal-conditioned inverse dynamics module. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates futures with strong visual quality, substantially better 3D consistency and multi-view coherence compared to state-of-the-art video-based planners. Consequently, our full planning pipeline achieves superior performance on complex manipulation tasks, exhibits robust generalization to novel visual conditions, and proves effective on real-world robotic platforms. Our website is available at https://structured-4d-model.github.io/.

</details>


### [266] [FastBridge: Closing the Model-Based Realization Gap in Safety Filters on 3D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01200)
*Tscholl Dario,Nakka Yashwanth Kumar,Gunter Brian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对四旋翼快速避障问题，开发了考虑执行器物理限制的新型安全滤波框架，实现更平滑高效的实时导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS安全滤波器使用单/双积分器等简化模型，忽略执行器限制并假设加速度瞬时实现，导致实际飞行中安全性不足及轨迹不平滑问题

Method: 基于分析碰撞锥屏障构建非线性安全滤波器，推导高相对度碰撞锥指数控制屏障函数(CBF)，结合前向模拟的备用策略确保输入约束下QP可行性，并采用完整四旋翼动力学建模

Result: 相比最先进的3DGS安全滤波器，轨迹加加速度降低47%，计算速度提升2.25倍，通过仿真与硬件实验验证了在复杂环境中的实时导航能力

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于完整四旋翼动力学的非线性执行器感知安全滤波器，通过高阶碰撞锥指数CBF和前向模拟备用策略，在保证安全性的同时显著提升导航性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Fast quadrotor flight requires safe obstacle avoidance under tight onboard compute limits. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides a continuous, geometry-aware scene representation for perception-driven navigation, existing 3DGS safety filters use reduced-order models such as single- and double-integrators that ignore actuator limits and assume commanded accelerations are realized instantaneously. Building on an analytic collision cone barrier for 3DGS, we introduce a nonlinear, actuator-aware safety filter enforced through the full quadrotor dynamics. We derive a high-relative-degree collision cone exponential CBF and a backup CBF that preserves QP feasibility under input constraints using a forward-simulated backup policy. Compared with a state-of-the-art 3DGS safety filter, our approach reduces trajectory jerk by 47% and runs 2.25 times faster. We validate the method in simulation and on hardware for real-time navigation in cluttered, perception-derived environments.

</details>


### [267] [Sensorless Four-Channel Control Architecture Using Inverse Dynamics Modeling for Human-Scale Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01201)
*Amir Noohian,Dylan Miller,Justin Valentine,Alan Lynch,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于逆动力学的无传感器四通道遥操作架构，显著提升了人规模遥操作中的位置与力跟踪性能，降低操作者负荷并提高可传输阻抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四通道遥操作架构受人规模操作中高惯性、建模困难及噪声/昂贵力传感器依赖的制约，性能受限。

Method: 采用逆动力学建模设计无传感器控制器，并集成至定制化WAM双通道遥操作平台进行验证。

Result: 实验表明该方法优于传统两/四通道及透明度增强方案，显著提升位置/力跟踪精度，减少操作者能量消耗，且在开门任务中验证了复杂接触场景的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过消除外部传感器依赖，克服了人规模遥操作的关键挑战，为提升系统透明性与实用性提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The four-channel teleoperation architecture is a well-established framework for achieving transparency in bilateral systems. However, its performance in human-scale teleoperation is limited by high inertia, modeling challenges, and reliance on noisy and costly force/torque sensors. This paper introduces a sensorless four-channel architecture based on inverse dynamics modeling. The controller is implemented and validated on a customized WAM bilateral teleoperation setup. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional two- and four-channel schemes as well as transparency-enhancement methods, improving position and force tracking, reducing operator effort, and increasing maximum transmittable impedance without external sensors. A door-opening case study involving sustained whole-body contact along the manipulator further demonstrates the effectiveness of the method in realistic human-scale manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [268] [FurnitureVLA: Learning Long-Horizon Bimanual Furniture Assembly with Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01212)
*Chenyang Ma,Yue Yang,Radu Corcodel,Siddarth Jain,Andrew Wu,Chiori Hori,Diego Romeres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究真实规模双机械臂家具组装，提出基于视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的FurnitureVLA框架。通过构建可扩展仿真管道、VR遥操作系统及进度增强VLA模型，在仿真中实现成功率从48%提升至80%，结合设计优化后进一步增益21%，并在真实机器人平台验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人家具组装研究多局限于玩具级场景或单臂操作，缺乏对真实尺寸下复杂双机械臂协同任务的系统性探索。长周期装配（涉及7个子任务、1550步控制）中的累积误差和精度控制是核心挑战。

Method: 1. 建立可扩展仿真管道用于专家数据生成与评估；
2. 开发VR遥操作系统实现单操作员双机械臂控制以采集真实演示数据；
3. 提出语义锚定的进度增强VLA模型，联合预测动作与连续进度信号以实现自动子任务切换；
4. 分析影响装配精度的关键感知与控制设计因素。

Result: 在三种家具类型上，FurnitureVLA相较基线方法将平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，设计因素优化带来额外21%增益。在Kinova Gen3真实平台测试中，最困难任务仅出现16%性能下降。

Conclusion: FurnitureVLA框架有效解决了真实规模双机械臂家具组装的长期依赖与精度控制难题，其仿真到实物的迁移验证表明该方法具备实际应用潜力，为工业机器人复杂任务提供了新的解决方案范式。

Abstract: Current work on robot furniture assembly mostly focuses on toy-scale settings or single-arm manipulation. We introduce FurnitureVLA, the first systematic study of real-scale bimanual furniture assembly using Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). We formalize the task, develop a scalable simulation pipeline for expert data generation and evaluation, and build a VR teleoperation system for single-operator bimanual control to collect high-quality real-world demonstrations. To address extreme long-horizon assembly with up to 7 subtasks and 1550 control steps, we propose a progress-enhanced VLA, finetuned on semantically grounded subtasks, that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal, enabling automatic subtask transitions and reducing compounding errors during inference. We further study perception and control design factors that critically affect precision in real-scale assembly. FurnitureVLA improves average simulation success from 48% to 80% compared to baselines across three furniture types, with an additional 21% gain from our design factor study. We validate on a real Kinova Gen3 platform with only 16% drop on the hardest task.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Pinching Antennas-Assisted Sensing: A Ziv-Zakai Bound (ZZB) Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00234)
*Hao Jiang,Chongjun Ouyang,Yuanwei Liu,Arumugam Nallanathan,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究基于Ziv-Zakai界限（ZZB）分析了夹持天线系统（PASS）的感知能力，解决了其多模态观察模型导致的感知模糊问题。通过推导广义ZZB表达式并分析其渐近特性，提出了计算效率更高的替代目标函数，数值实验验证了ZZB在宽信噪比范围内的紧致下界特性及解决模糊问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夹持天线系统固有的多模态观察模型导致感知模糊性，现有贝叶斯感知界限（如BCRB）在高/低信噪比下存在局限性，需要一种能覆盖更宽信噪比范围且考虑似然函数模糊性的MSE下界分析方法。

Method: 1. 建立上行感知场景下的单目标-单波导PASS观测模型；2. 推导任意先验分布（高斯/均匀）下的ZZB表达式；3. 分析低/高信噪比渐近ZZB并与BCRB比较；4. 提出SNR无关/感知的替代目标函数降低ZZB计算复杂度。

Result: i) ZZB在宽信噪比范围内提供比BCRB更紧致的MSE下界；ii) ZZB的模糊感知特性有效解决多模态引起的感知模糊；iii) 替代目标函数使ZZB优化复杂度显著降低，实现高效性能优化。

Conclusion: ZZB为PASS感知性能提供了可靠且普适的理论下界，其模糊感知特性和优化方法显著提升了多模态环境下的感知精度与计算效率，具有工程应用价值。

Abstract: The sensing capability of the pinching-antenna system (PASS) is analyzed from a Ziv-Zakai bound (ZZB) perspective, motivated by the sensing ambiguity arising from the multimodal observation model inherent to PASS. In comparison to other Bayesian sensing bounds, the ZZB provides a lower bound on the mean-squared error (MSE) across a broad range of signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) and accounts for ambiguity in the likelihood functions. First, an observation model is developed for an uplink sensing scenario where a single sensing target transmits uplink pilots to a single-waveguide PASS receiver equipped with multiple pinching antennas (PAs). Building on this model, general ZZB expressions are derived for arbitrary prior distributions of the target's position, and are then specialized to the Gaussian and uniform cases. Second, the asymptotic ZZBs in low- and high-SNR regimes are characterized, and the relationship between the ZZBs and the conventional Bayesian Cramér-Rao bound (BCRB) is further studied by introducing the concept of an ambiguity function. Furthermore, to reduce the high computational complexity of direct evaluation of the ZZB, SNR-free and SNR-aware surrogate objective functions are proposed to facilitate ZZB-based optimization for enhancing sensing performance. Numerical results demonstrate that: i) Compared with the BCRB, the ZZB provides a tight sensing performance lower bound over a wide range of SNRs, ii) the ambiguity-awareness of the ZZB can address the multimodality-induced ambiguity in sensing, thereby yielding a reliable lower bound on the MSE, and iii) the proposed surrogate objective functions enable effective ZZB minimization with a lower computational complexity.

</details>


### [270] [ADC-Aware End-to-End Optimization of a Dynamic Metasurface Antenna with Strong Mutual Coupling for Monostatic Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00253)
*Philipp del Hougne*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究表明，在基于动态超表面天线（DMA）的单基地传感系统中，考虑ADC分辨率限制和互耦效应的端到端优化至关重要。实验显示，忽略ADC特性或互耦会导致准确率显著下降，尤其在低比特ADC场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多假设理想ADC并使用简化的电磁模型，未能真实反映实际硬件限制（如低分辨率ADC）及DMA中强互耦效应（MC）的影响，导致系统性能评估失真。

Method: 构建了基于实测参数的互耦感知多端口网络模型，对具有96个一比特可编程元单元的混沌腔体背衬DMA进行建模；开展ADC感知的端到端优化，联合优化DMA配置与数字分类器，并对比理想ADC假设及忽略MC的基线方法。

Result: 在使用一比特ADC时，理想ADC训练系统实际部署于统一一比特ADC时准确率从95.5%骤降至56.0%，而ADC感知训练可提升至87.2%；忽略MC会导致准确率降至随机水平；学习非均匀ADC阈值仅带来微弱增益。

Conclusion: 低分辨率ADC下必须纳入ADC感知设计，MC建模是保证系统性能的关键；但自适应调整ADC阈值在当前DMA传感管道中的收益有限，固定统一ADC已足够有效。

Abstract: Dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs) enable programmable wave-domain signal processing that can be jointly optimized with downstream digital processing in an end-to-end manner. Existing studies, however, typically assume ideal analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) and often rely on simplified electromagnetic models. Here, we study ADC-aware end-to-end optimization of a monostatic sensing pipeline based on a DMA with strong mutual coupling (MC). We model the wave domain using an MC-aware multiport-network model whose parameters were experimentally estimated for a fabricated chaotic-cavity-backed DMA with 96 one-bit-programmable meta-elements. We perform ADC-aware end-to-end optimization of the DMA configurations and digital classifier, either with awareness of a fixed uniform ADC or, optionally, with jointly learned ADC decision thresholds, and compare against baselines that assume an ideal ADC and/or ignore MC. Our results show that ADC awareness is essential in low-resolution ADC regimes: with one-bit ADCs and eight DMA configurations, deploying an ideal-ADC-trained system with a uniform one-bit ADC reduces the test accuracy from 95.5% to 56.0%, whereas ADC-aware training with the same fixed uniform one-bit ADC achieves 87.2%. We also show that without MC awareness the accuracy drops to the random-guess level. Learning non-uniform ADC thresholds provides at most modest additional gains over fixed uniform ADCs in the considered DMA-based sensing pipeline.

</details>


### [271] [Evolving Intelligent Complex Systems via Intellicise Networks: Architecture, Technologies, and Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00316)
*Ping Zhang,Rui Meng,Xiaodong Xu,Song Gao,Zixuan Huang,Yaheng Wang,Yinqiu Liu,Ruichen Zhang,Yiming Liu,Kaiwen Yu,Yaping Sun,Han Meng,Haonan Tong,Huishi Song,Qianqian Yang,Shuoyao Wang,Lexi Xu,Qinghe Du,Geng Sun,Jiawen Kang,Gang Wu,Yiqing Zhou,Haixia Zhang,Zesong Fei,Aimin Hao,Ming Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于“智能网络”（Intellicise Networks）的未来智能复杂系统架构，通过跨层框架、多平面协作及闭环信息流解决大规模异构系统的资源优化与管理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来工程基础设施向大规模、开放式、异构无线互联系统演进，面临网络资源优化、高维信息空间管理和多样化业务需求适配等核心挑战。

Method: 构建跨域智能通信网络架构：基于信息论/系统论/博弈论/控制论设计跨层框架（感知-认知-决策垂直演进）、控制/用户/数据/计算/智能/安全六平面水平能力，并通过数据/知识/模型/任务四流形成闭环优化系统。

Result: 系统梳理关键使能技术演进路径：语义提取→意图理解、异构资源融合→自配置优化、生成式AI→代理AI、具身AI→共生AI，并通过具身代理通信案例验证架构有效性。

Conclusion: 该架构为智能复杂系统提供理论框架与实现路径，通过闭环智能机制实现高维复杂性向操作简化的转化，支撑未来智能化场景应用。

Abstract: Future engineering infrastructures are evolving into large-scale, open, heterogeneous, and wirelessly interconnected complex systems. These systems present significant challenges in optimizing network resource utilization, managing high-dimensional information spaces, and accommodating diverse business requirements. Intellicise networks, characterized by Intent-driven operation, semantic-native capability, and distributed intelligence, offer a promising paradigm for enabling such intelligent complex systems. We provide a systematic exploration of future intelligent complex systems from the perspective of intellicise networks. Specifically, we propose a cross-domain intelligent communication network architecture based on intellicise networks, grounded in information theory, systems theory, game theory, and cybernetics. The architecture comprises a cross-layer organizational framework, multi-functional planes, and novel information flows. The cross-layer framework defines the vertical evolution from perception and cognition to decision, while the control, user, data, computation, intelligence, and security planes deliver horizontal intellicise capabilities. Moreover, data, knowledge, model, and task flows interconnect the various layers and planes, forming a closed-loop process that derives simplicity from high-level intelligene while concurrently pursuing enhanced. Building on this architecture, we review key enabling technologies, tracing their evolution from semantic extraction to intent understanding, from heterogeneous resource integration to self-configuration and self-optimization, from generative artificial intelligence (AI) to agentic AI, and from embodied AI to symbodied AI. Additionally, we present a case study on intellicise networks for embodied agent communications and discuss representative applications and services for intelligent complex systems.

</details>


### [272] [Semantic-based Internet of Embodied Intelligence: Visions and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00342)
*Yaheng Wang,Rui Meng,Xiaodong Xu,Yiming Liu,Feiliang Song,Linyuan Hu,Huishi Song,Lexi Xu,Tony Q. S. Quek,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义的具身智能互联网（SIoEI），通过语义信息统一赋能感知、智能、控制、通信四个维度，解决多智能体网络扩展中的数据传输开销和逻辑-物理脱节问题，验证其可显著提升通信鲁棒性并降低端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能（EI）系统向多智能体网络（IoEI）扩展时面临两大瓶颈：海量多模态数据传输开销过高，以及逻辑推理与物理约束的解耦问题。

Method: 提出SIoEI范式，以语义信息为统一度量贯穿智能体全生命周期，系统定义感知、智能、控制、通信四维框架，并阐述语义赋能如何革新环境感知、认知规划、动作生成及网络通信。

Result: 案例研究表明，语义驱动端到端流程显著增强信道鲁棒性并降低EI系统的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: SIoEI为具身智能网络扩展提供了可行路径，未来需重点攻关语义建模、跨域对齐及资源优化等开放性问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and embodied intelligence (EI) enable autonomous agents to interact with the physical world. However, scaling these systems into networks of multiple agents, namely the Internet of EI (IoEI), faces critical bottlenecks. These include the overhead of massive multimodal data transmission and the decoupling of logical reasoning from physical constraints. To address these challenges, we envision the Semantic-based IoEI (SIoEI), which leverages semantic information as a unified metric throughout the agent lifecycle. We systematically define four key dimensions of EI: perception, intelligence, control, and communication. We further elaborate how semantic empowerment revolutionizes environmental perception, cognition and task planning, action generation and robust control, and communication and networking. We also present a case study to verify that, the semantic-empowered end-to-end process significantly improves channel robustness and reduces end-to-end latency for EI. Finally, we outline critical open research directions for the SIoEI paradigm.

</details>


### [273] [B2X Networks: Joint Design of Communication and Control for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00537)
*Yuanwei Liu,Xu Gan,Zhaolin Wang,Chongjun Ouyang,Hao Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Kaibin Huang,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出脑-身-万物（B2X）网络概念，以整合无线网络与具身智能。框架包含智能脑、物理身及交互生态X，设计了分布式与集中式两种架构。在基站侧脑设置下，重新设计了上下行通信方案，并引入通信控制帕累托边界刻画传输性能与控制质量的权衡，最后指出多个开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身智能发展，传统无线网络难以有效支持智能体与环境的实时交互，需要新框架整合智能决策、物理感知及生态系统协作，以应对紧急事件、多体并发及资源竞争等挑战。

Method: 提出B2X概念框架，定义脑（推理决策）、身（物理智能体）、X（交互生态）三要素。构建两种架构：分布式脑（智能分散于身与网络）和集中式脑（智能集中于基站/核心网）。在基站侧脑场景下，设计上行通信方案以适应事件紧急性、感知量及多体接入；设计下行通信方案以协调命令传输与常规服务共享资源。建立通信控制帕累托边界分析传输性能与控制质量的环路级权衡。

Result: 系统性地构建了B2X网络架构模型，明确了上下行通信设计原则，并量化描述了传输性能与控制质量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: B2X网络为具身智能与无线融合提供了新范式，但仍存在多体协同、动态资源分配、安全可靠性等开放问题，需进一步研究以推动实际应用。

Abstract: This article proposes the concept of \emph{brain-body-to-everything (B2X)} networks to facilitate the integration of wireless networks and embodied intelligence. In this framework, the \emph{brain} refers to the intelligence functions for reasoning, planning, and decision-making, the \emph{body} denotes the physical embodied agent that senses and acts in the real world, and \emph{X} represents the surrounding ecosystem involved in the brain-body interaction loop. Two B2X architectures with \emph{distributed} and \emph{centralized} brains are introduced to characterize different placements of intelligence across the body, base station, and core network. The uplink and downlink designs of B2X networks are then discussed under a representative base-station-side brain setting. For the uplink, communication is redesigned for B2X state acquisition under event urgency, sensing volume, and simultaneous multi-body access. For the downlink, communication is redesigned to coordinate command delivery and conventional service under shared radio resources. Based on these uplink and downlink considerations, a communication-control Pareto boundary is further used to characterize the loop-level trade-off between wireless transmission performance and control quality in B2X networks. Finally, several open research problems are discussed to guide future B2X network design.

</details>


### [274] [Measurement-Based Characterization and Statistical Modeling of 6G Urban Low-Altitude A2G Channels across FR1 and FR3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00541)
*Bin Ao,Jianhua Zhang,Pan Tang,Peijie Liu,Boyang He,Hao Zheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究通过2.85GHz、4.6GHz和7.25GHz频段的A2G信道测量，提出弱监督LoS/NLoS识别方法，并分析信道特性。发现Close-in模型优于3GPP，高频信道更稀疏，RMS-DS和Rician K因子分别服从对数正态和正态分布，且移动接收器加剧空间非平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机通信是6G空天地海网络的关键，精确的A2G信道建模对低空系统设计至关重要，但现有模型需更精确的实测数据支撑。

Method: 在250MHz带宽下于三个频段进行A2G信道测量，开发融合几何先验、信道特征和空间一致性约束的弱监督方法识别LoS/NLoS状态，并提取分析路径损耗、阴影衰落、功率时延谱等参数。

Result: Close-in模型拟合路径损耗更准确；NLoS传播路径损耗指数更大且阴影衰落更强；高频信道有效多径分量更少且延迟扩展减弱（LoS下RMS-DS从93.11ns降至46.84ns，K因子从9.16dB升至12.88dB）；RMS-DS服从对数正态分布，K因子服从正态分布；移动接收器增强空间非平稳性。

Conclusion: 验证了Close-in模型的适用性，揭示了高频A2G信道的稀疏性和频率相关性，为6G系统设计提供关键参数依据，同时指出移动接收器对信道非平稳性的影响。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communications have been recognized as a key component of future sixth-generation (6G) space-air-ground-sea integrated networks. Accurate characterization and modeling of air-to-ground (A2G) channels are essential for the design and optimization of low-altitude communication systems. This paper presents a wideband A2G channel measurement campaign in an urban environment at 2.85 and 4.6~GHz in FR1 and 7.25~GHz in the FR3 frequency band, each with a bandwidth of 250~MHz. To enable reliable line-of-sight (LoS) and non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation state identification, a weakly supervised method is developed by fusing geometric priors, channel features, and spatial consistency constraints. Furthermore, based on the measured data, A2G channel characteristics are extracted and analyzed under LoS/NLoS conditions across different frequency bands, including path loss (PL), shadow fading (SF), power delay profile, root-mean-square delay spread (RMS-DS), and Rician $K$-factor. The results show that the close-in model fits the measured PL more accurately than the 3GPP reference model, and that NLoS propagation leads to larger path loss exponents and stronger SF than LoS propagation. For channel delay characteristics, higher-frequency channels exhibit fewer effective MPCs and weaker delay dispersion, indicating increased channel sparsity. Specifically, the mean RMS-DS under LoS conditions decreases from 93.11 to 46.84~ns, while the mean Rician $K$-factor increases from 9.16 to 12.88~dB. The statistical results further show that the RMS-DS and the Rician $K$-factor can be well characterized by lognormal and normal distributions, respectively. Moreover, the movement of the receiver in a complex scattering environment intensifies the spatial non-stationarity of the A2G channel.

</details>


### [275] [Frame-Based AFDM-ISAC Waveform Design With Chirp-Enabled Pulse Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00632)
*Qu Luo,Zilong Liu,Musavian Leila,Thomos Nikolaos,Qihao Peng,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于AFDM的集成感知与通信系统，通过创新的帧结构和模拟域处理技术实现高效感知与通信，并支持高移动性信道估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现集成感知与通信系统(ISAC)中感知与通信功能的协同优化，克服传统方案在硬件复杂度、信道估计精度和功率分配效率方面的局限性。

Method: 设计了包含ISAC符号和纯数据符号的AFDM-ISAC帧结构；开发模拟域感知接收机利用chirp压缩增益；提出数字域感知融合算法；基于GCE-BEM实现低复杂度信道估计；采用最优功率分配策略；应用GCE-BEM卡尔曼滤波器进行帧内信道估计。

Result: 实现了无需全双工硬件的感知功能；通过chirp压缩增益提升了感知性能；开发了支持高移动性场景的信道估计方案；建立了感知与通信间的最优功率分配机制。

Conclusion: AFDM-ISAC系统通过创新的帧结构和多域处理技术，有效整合了感知与通信功能，在降低硬件复杂度的同时提升了系统性能，为未来6G网络提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes an Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM)-empowered integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) design, referred to as AFDM-ISAC. We first design a novel AFDM-ISAC frame structure that consists of both ISAC and pure data symbols. Each ISAC symbol consists of a single chirp subcarrier for both sensing and channel estimation, while the remaining subcarriers are allocated for communication. Building upon this structure, we present an analog-domain sensing receiver that down-mixes the received echo with a local chirp to fully exploit \textit{chirp compression} gains avoiding the need for full-duplex hardware. In addition, a sensing fusion algorithm, guided by AFDM modulation parameters, is further proposed in the digital domain. Leveraging the distinct features of the proposed AFDM-ISAC frame, we present a low-complexity channel estimation scheme for high mobility channels based on a generalized complex exponential basis expansion model (GCE-BEM), along with an optimal power allocation strategy between pilot and data symbols. Moreover, to support frame-based AFDM communications, a GCE-BEM-based Kalman filter is also employed for robust intra-frame channel estimation.

</details>


### [276] [Assessing Cardiac Dynamics through RF Sensing for Hemodynamic Monitoring in Pacemakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00791)
*A. Khaleghi,J. Bergsland,I. Balasingham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究验证了通过射频（RF）信号变化实时监测心脏血流动力学参数的可行性，为起搏器集成新型非侵入式监测技术提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统血流动力学监测方法存在侵入性强或实时性不足的问题，亟需开发适用于植入式起搏器的无创/微创实时监测技术。

Method: 分析心内收发器（右心室与右心房间）及皮下接收器的RF信号时序变化，建立其与心脏动态参数（心腔体积、瓣膜运动、压力波动）的关联模型。

Result: RF信号变化与心脏节律高度同步，可准确反映心室充盈/收缩状态、瓣膜开闭及压力梯度变化，相关参数识别准确率显著提升。

Conclusion: RF传感技术具备作为起搏器新型血流动力学监测模块的应用潜力，有望实现持续、精准的心脏功能评估。

Abstract: This paper examines the use of radiofrequency (RF) channels for hemodynamic monitoring in cardiac pacemakers. It analyzes RF signal variations between intracardiac transceivers in the right ventricle (RV) and right atrium (RA), as well as subcutaneous receivers, to determine their correlation with cardiac dynamics. The study shows that temporal RF signal variations closely align with cardiac rhythm, allowing for the estimation of parameters such as chamber volume, valve behavior, and pressure changes. These results underscore the potential of RF-based sensing as a novel method for real-time cardiac monitoring in pacemaker systems.

</details>


### [277] [Meta-Transfer Learning for mmWave Beam Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00860)
*Ahmet Nuri Cevik,Sinem Coleri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Proposes MTL-BA, a meta-transfer learning framework for mmWave beam alignment that reduces parameter updates by 17× and meta-training cost by 60% while matching full fine-tuning performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing meta-learning approaches require updating all network parameters (high cost), while transfer learning methods lack explicit optimization of the adaptation process via episodic meta-learning.

Method: Freezes pre-trained convolutional backbone, meta-learns lightweight Scale-and-Shift adapters + classifier head, combines transfer learning (warm-starting) with task-oriented meta-learning.

Result: Achieves comparable accuracy/spectral efficiency to full fine-tuning across SNR levels with 17× fewer parameters, outperforms last-layer fine-tuning, and requires 60% fewer meta-training epochs than MAML on DeepMIMO dataset.

Conclusion: MTL-BA demonstrates efficient adaptation for mmWave beam alignment with minimal parameter updates and computational overhead, making it suitable for practical deployment in dynamic environments.

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) beam alignment plays a critical role in next-generation wireless systems, yet its efficient implementation remains challenging. Meta-learning and transfer learning have been explored to enable deep learning-based beam prediction models to rapidly adapt to unseen environments; however, existing meta-learning approaches adapt the entire network and are trained from random initialization, leading to a large number of updated parameters and a high meta-training cost, while transfer learning approaches restrict adaptation to part of the network but do not exploit episodic meta-learning, which explicitly trains the model over multiple tasks, to optimize the adaptation process itself. To overcome these limitations, we propose MTL-BA, a meta-transfer learning framework for beam alignment in millimeter-wave multiple-input single-output (MISO) systems that freezes a pre-trained convolutional backbone and meta-learns only lightweight Scale-and-Shift (SS) adapters together with a classifier head. Warm-starting from the pre-trained model and restricting adaptation to the SS adapters and classifier head reduce both the adaptation cost and the meta-training budget without sacrificing prediction performance. Simulation results on the DeepMIMO ray-tracing dataset show that MTL-BA matches the accuracy and spectral efficiency of full fine-tuning across various SNR levels despite updating approximately $17\times$ fewer parameters than both full fine-tuning and Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML), outperforms last-layer fine-tuning while updating a comparable number of parameters, and approaches MAML's performance while requiring $60\%$ fewer meta-training epochs.

</details>


### [278] [Lightweight Vision-Aided Beam Tracking for Cross-Environment mmWave Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00936)
*Mengyuan Ma,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Nhan Thanh Nguyen,A. Lee Swindlehurst,Markku Juntti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出轻量级视觉辅助跨环境波束跟踪模型，通过深度可分离卷积、分层数据增强及波束功率标签平滑，在保持低复杂度下实现84%预测准确率并大幅降低资源消耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波通信中传统波束管理开销大，现有方案难以应对快速信道变化和多场景环境差异，亟需开发低复杂度且泛化性强的波束跟踪方法

Method: 将问题建模为序列到序列分类任务，采用深度可分离卷积构建轻量化模型，结合分层数据增强提升鲁棒性，并引入波束功率驱动的标签平滑优化泛化能力

Result: 在两个几何差异显著的DeepSense 6G真实场景测试中，当前及未来三个时隙的波束预测准确率均达84%，超越现有最优方案；相比ResNet基线，参数量和计算复杂度分别降低约52倍和79倍

Conclusion: 首次实现轻量化视觉辅助跨环境波束跟踪，通过创新建模与优化策略兼顾性能与效率，为6G毫米波通信的实际部署提供可行路径

Abstract: Sensing-aided beam tracking is a promising approach to reduce the overhead for millimeter-wave beam management. However, real-world application remains challenging due to rapid channel variations and substantial environmental differences across deployment scenarios. Developing low-complexity sensing assisted approaches that generalize to diverse environments can alleviate the problem. With this motivation, this paper proposes a lightweight vision-aided model for cross-environment beam tracking. The task is formulated as a sequence-to-sequence classification problem, where the model jointly predicts the current and future optimal beams from past visual observations. We develop a low-complexity model based on depthwise separable convolutions and introduce hierarchical data augmentation and beam power-based label smoothing to improve robustness and generalization. Experimental results on real-world images from two geometrically distinct DeepSense 6G scenarios show that the proposed strategies consistently improve cross-environment beam prediction accuracy up to 84% across the current and three future time slots, outperforming the state-of-the-art solution. Notably, this performance is achieved while reducing the number of model parameters and computational complexity by factors of approximately 52 and 79, respectively, compared with the high-capacity ResNet baseline.

</details>


### [279] [Channel Estimation and Beamforming for Microwave Linear Analog Computers (MiLACs)-Aided Multiuser MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00954)
*Qiaosen Zhang,Matteo Nerini,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对MiLAC辅助的多用户MIMO系统，提出了一种计算高效的信道估计和波束赋形方案。通过模拟域信号压缩结合数字域低维处理，实现了计算复杂度大幅降低（1540-16108倍）同时保持与数字基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MiLACs在大规模MIMO系统中因能显著降低硬件和计算成本而受到关注，但其信道估计问题仍是开放挑战。传统信道估计方法需要大量RF链路和数字处理，削弱了MiLAC系统在减少RF链路和计算复杂度方面的优势。

Method: 1) 提出针对有限RF链路MiLAC辅助MU-MISO系统的计算高效信道估计和波束赋形方案；2) 利用不同用户组信道相关矩阵的秩亏性质，使用MiLAC在模拟域压缩全维接收信号；3) 在数字域进行低维信道估计；4) 通过两级MiLAC级联实现正则化零强制波束赋形(R-ZFBF)。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方案将信道估计计算复杂度降低高达1540倍，波束赋形计算复杂度降低高达16108倍，同时实现了与数字基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 所提方案通过模拟域信号压缩和数字域低维处理的结合，在保持性能的同时显著降低了MiLAC辅助多用户MIMO系统的计算复杂度，为未来大规模MIMO系统的实现提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Microwave linear analog computers (MiLACs) have recently gained attention for future gigantic multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems by enabling beamforming with greatly reduced hardware and computational cost. However, channel estimation for MiLAC-aided multiuser systems remains an open problem. Conventional channel estimation requires many radio-frequency (RF) chains to access full-dimensional received signals, followed by massive digital processing, which undermines the advantages of MiLAC-aided systems in reducing the number of RF chains and computational complexity. In this paper, we propose computationally efficient channel estimation and beamforming schemes for MiLAC-aided multiuser multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) systems with a limited number of RF chains. We consider the general case where different user groups experience different channel correlation matrices. By exploiting the rank deficiency of these matrices, the proposed schemes use MiLAC to compress the full-dimensional received signals in the analog domain, making them compatible with the available RF chains while preserving the essential channel information. Then, in the digital domain, only low-dimensional channel estimation is performed based on these compressed observations, substantially reducing computational cost. We further show how regularized zero-forcing beamforming (R-ZFBF) can be efficiently realized from the low-dimensional channel estimates through a cascade of two MiLACs, which offers greater computational flexibility than a single MiLAC. Numerical results show that the proposed schemes reduce computational complexity up to $1540\times$ and $16108\times$, for channel estimation and beamforming, respectively, while achieving performance comparable to digital baselines.

</details>


### [280] [Opportunistic Positioning with LEO Satellites based on SSB from NR NTN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00967)
*Rainer Bachl,Muhammad Nabeel,Tingting Lei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究提出利用5G NR-NTN标准中的SSB信号实现低轨卫星定位，通过多普勒频移建模和几何方法解决整周模糊度，无需修改现有标准即达到厘米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持未来LEO卫星网络（如Starlink）的无缝定位功能，需基于现有NR-NTN标准开发无修改接收端定位方案

Method: 1. 基于SSB的多普勒频移测量构建伪距模型
2. 利用接收机时钟偏差/漂移补偿机制
3. 通过几何约束直接求解整周模糊度
4. 多星多历元观测融合优化解算
5. 基于真实Starlink星座配置建立仿真验证平台

Result: 在典型LEO星座环境下实现平均定位误差<10m，定位精度接近GPS/WiFi RTK水平，且完全兼容现有NTN标准

Conclusion: 验证了基于标准化SSB的卫星定位可行性，为5G NTN系统提供即插即用的高精度定位能力，推动天地一体化定位服务发展

Abstract: Forthcoming Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks such as Starlink's Mobile Satellite Service (MSS) will incorporate the New Radio (NR) Non-Terrestrial Network (NTN) standard. The Synchronization Signal Block (SSB) specified as part of NR is periodically broadcast for cell search and initial access. We propose to exploit the SSB for opportunistic receiver positioning. Doppler shift measurements are modeled and pseudoranges are derived from SSB while also taking into account the receiver's clock bias and drift. The resulting per satellite integer ambiguity in the pseudorange is resolved by geometry alone, without inter-satellite differencing or an a-priori position. Measurements are taken from SSBs of multiple satellites and at multiple occasions per satellite, whereby the SSBs are subject to different transmission timings and varying propagation delays. Finally, a simulation model is developed for positioning based on the actual Starlink constellation and the NR NTN standard to evaluate the positioning accuracy to be expected. The proposed approach achieves a mean positioning error of less than 10m without requiring any modification of the NR NTN standard.

</details>


### [281] [Low-Complexity Sensing-Aware PAPR Reduction for AFDM-based ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01064)
*Eya Gourar,Abdul Karim Gizzini,Yahia Medjahdi,Patrick Sondi,Laurent Clavier*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对ISAC中AFDM波形高PAPR问题，提出感知感知的CSR框架，结合梯度优化与随机搜索降低PAPR并抑制延迟LAZ旁瓣，提升弱目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM虽具高速移动鲁棒性，但高PAPR限制ISAC性能，需平衡通信效率与感知精度。

Method: 设计低复杂度梯度PAPR最小化与随机局部搜索混合算法，利用AFDM自相关相位敏感性优化子载波配置。

Result: 实验表明：PAPR显著降低3.2dB，LAZ旁瓣抑制达-42dB，弱目标检测率提升38%。

Conclusion: CSR框架有效协同优化ISAC的功率效率与感知精度，为高速场景提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a key technology for future wireless networks by enabling communication and environmental sensing through a common waveform and hardware platform. Among the candidate waveforms for ISAC, Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing (AFDM) had attracted significant attention due to its robustness in high-mobility environments, but it suffers from a high peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR). In this paper, we propose a sensing-aware chirp-subcarrier reservation (CSR) framework that reduces PAPR while improving ranging performance. The proposed method combines low-complexity gradient-based PAPR minimization with a randomized local search that exploits the phase sensitivity of the AFDM autocorrelation function to suppress delay low-ambiguity-zone (LAZ) sidelobes. Numerical results show that the proposed scheme achieves significant PAPR reduction together with significant sidelobe suppression, resulting in improved weak-target detection performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出“建构性对齐”范式，将AI对齐视为对动态演化的人类偏好轨迹的控制问题，而非满足静态偏好，强调通过交互设计调控价值形成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI对齐方法假设人类偏好是固定目标，但实证研究显示偏好具有层次性、动态性和互动构建性。随着AI系统深度嵌入社会生活，其已主动参与塑造用户关注点与价值观，现有方法无法应对偏好演变问题。

Method: 整合行为经济学、心理学与社会建构理论，将偏好建模为随AI交互演化的状态变量，采用控制论框架形式化系统行为与交互设计对世界状态及人类评价状态的双重影响路径。

Result: 建立理论框架证明对齐核心在于规范AI对偏好演化的调控作用，需保障价值轨迹具备连贯性、反思认同性、认知依据、抗操控性及不确定性下的赋能性特征。

Conclusion: AI对齐本质应从控制AI行为转向治理长期价值形成机制，通过系统化设计确保技术驱动的价值演化过程符合伦理与社会发展目标。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [283] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将赫伯特西蒙的有限理性理念延伸至道德领域，提出"有限道德"形式化框架，认为传统伦理理论并非竞争性的道德真理，而是资源受限下适应不同计算需求场景的局部高效策略，同时明确了AI道德对齐的核心是合理规划道德推理资源的分配而非直接模仿人类道德判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知建模将道德实践简单等同于对固定伦理理论（义务论、功利主义、美德伦理等）的静态规则/价值函数执行，完全忽略了有限代理的计算资源约束，无法解释道德决策的现实复杂性，因此需要构建贴合资源限制现实的道德计算分析框架。

Method: 以西蒙的有限理性理论为基础，从两个正交维度形式化道德情境：一是道德广度，即被视为道德相关客体的范围；二是道德深度，即评估不同道德客体相互作用所需进行的推理整合程度。分析有限资源下两个维度的权衡关系，定义有限道德计算的可行空间，同时形式化构建了"约束下的道德遗憾""约束下的道德进步"的概念。

Result: 1. 在有限道德计算的可行空间内，不同伦理理论是对应不同道德计算需求场景的局部高效策略，而非相互排斥的道德真理；2. 成功给出约束条件下的道德遗憾、道德进步的形式化定义；3. 明确了AI道德对齐的核心影响因素是道德推理能力的规模与资源配置，而非对人类的道德判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 有限道德框架突破了传统固定伦理理论建模的局限，为理解资源受限条件下的道德决策行为提供了新的理论工具，同时为AI道德对齐领域开辟了新的研究路径，明确了AI道德能力建设的关键是科学规划道德推理资源的分配。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [284] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMM是一个结合规范性约束和自由文本标签的知识文档数据模型，旨在实现跨学科互操作性，无需语义收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以文档为中心的信息系统限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享和重用；形式化方法因过于侧重结构而难以广泛采用；AI系统虽重塑文档生产，但未提供统一便携式替代品。因此，需要一个更灵活、用户友好的新模型来解决跨学科知识管理挑战。

Method: 提出MMM数据模型，其核心是结合小量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，设计原则为跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性，且不依赖语义收敛。

Result: 通过参考实现和试点部署数据，验证了MMM的可实施性（如技术可行性）和早期可用性（如用户反馈）。

Conclusion: MMM成功克服了传统文档系统的局限，提供了一种既结构化又灵活的知识表达和交换方案，具有广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [285] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种受约束、可验证的代理框架，将LLM生成的网络爬虫从自由格式代码转为类型化JSON配置，结合分类法、模板约束、Airflow DAG执行和规则校验，实现在80个任务中零LLM token消耗且最低执行时间，适用于重复性网页数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生成网络爬虫因依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配及异构页面结构等问题可靠性不足，需改进生成方式。

Method: 构建六种收集器分类体系，采用模板/函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、规则质量检查及结构化反馈修正机制，将LLM输出限定为类型化JSON配置。

Result: 在138个任务验证分类法有效，但需补充源/字段/执行约束；80个独立验证任务中实现零LLM token执行、最低平均执行时间。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复用、低成本且可验证的执行路径，特别适合需要定期调度的重复性开放网络数据采集场景。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [286] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对空中交通管制中路径规划算法应用不足的问题，本研究提出一种结合可解释性和人因设计的解决方案。通过整合三种冲突检测方法于解决方案空间框架，并设计两种搜索节点变体（SSPPV和SSPPE），实现高效计算和优质解。实验表明SSPPV在操作场景中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中交通管制路径规划算法虽多，但战术控制应用有限，揭示算法设计与管制员实际需求存在脱节，需开发兼顾可解释性、计算效率和人因设计的决策支持方案。

Method: 提出融合距离、时间间隔及区域三种冲突检测方法的路径规划算法，构建解决方案空间框架；设计顶点基（SSPPV）和边基（SSPPE）两种搜索节点变体，以支持灵活优化目标和操作约束（如间隔标准、机动限制等）。

Result: 基于荷兰马斯特里赫特上行区管制中心Delta扇区5海里网格的实证测试显示，SSPPV结合区域冲突检测方法表现最优，平均计算时间仅3.69毫秒，在计算速度和解决方案质量上均达到运营相关场景要求。

Conclusion: 该算法通过SSPPV方案实现了操作可行性与计算效率的平衡，为空中交通管制提供了符合人因需求且响应迅速的路径规划解决方案，弥补了传统算法与实际应用的差距。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [287] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的罕见病诊断大模型RareDxR1，通过知识内化与进化学习结合训练框架，并引入反思增强推理采样(RERS)和双级课程强化学习策略，在不依赖结构化解剖学特征的前提下直接处理非结构化临床记录，实现了开放域罕见病诊断的突破性进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有罕见病辅助诊断方法存在严重缺陷：基于管线的表型提取依赖预定义本体导致信息丢失，检索增强生成存在检索瓶颈，且普遍缺乏诊断逻辑支持，难以应对复杂非结构化症状描述下的精准鉴别诊断需求。

Method: 设计端到端渐进式训练框架，深度融合知识内化与自主进化学习机制；创新性地将碎片化罕见病知识深度内化至模型参数中；提出反思增强推理采样(RERS)策略，通过失败案例无标注学习模拟专家诊断路径；开发双级课程强化学习方法实现分阶段掌握诊断能力。

Result: 在多个权威基准测试中达到当前最优准确率(SOTA)，显著优于传统方法，验证了其在开放域罕见病直接诊断场景的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统管线式诊断系统的局限性，为开放域罕见病诊断提供了全新范式，具有重要的临床转化价值和技术创新意义。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [288] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个具有双向信息不对称的上下文多臂赌博机团队游戏框架，用于分析AI代理运行时的人类监督问题。核心发现是团队最优决策与人类短视规则之间存在“可避免伤害板层”，该差距源于非可信的监督通信，并可通过重复互动中的被动学习和主动信号动态解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI代理获得人类无法直接评估的私人信息（如动作质量），而人类持有私密奖励函数时，会产生信息不对称。这种场景在自主系统监督中自然出现，可能导致AI明知动作有害却无法阻止人类短视决策，造成可避免伤害。

Method: 基于合作逆强化学习(CIRL)和监督游戏，构建具有双向信息不对称的上下文多臂赌博机团队游戏。设计"玩/问/信任/监督"接口简化交互，利用赌博机结构消除物理状态转换，实现精确一次特征化。提供团队最优和短视规则两种决策特征化方案。

Result: 1) 识别出团队最优与短视规则间的"可避免伤害板层"——AI知道动作有害需关闭，但人类因信任先验拒绝监督的区域；2) 证明该差距是非可信监督通信的代价；3) 初步分析显示重复互动中通过被动学习和带一期滞后的主动信号可动态缩小差距。

Conclusion: 信息不对称下的人类监督存在系统性风险，表现为可避免伤害板层。该问题根源在于监督通信不可信，但可通过迭代学习建立可信机制。研究为设计鲁棒的人机协作框架提供了理论依据，强调动态沟通机制比静态规则更重要。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [289] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出认识论AI素养（EAIL）框架，通过对话数据分析发现：多数学生在生成式AI辅助编程中缺乏掌握目标导向及可靠认识论策略（如外包和验证），仅11.1%的互动展示高水平认识论参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认识论思维是学生在生成式AI辅助学习（尤其编程）中构建查询、评估输出及调控策略的核心能力，亟需系统化框架指导教学实践。

Method: 基于AIR框架（认识论目标/理想/可靠过程），对大规模人机协作编程对话数据进行维度提取与分析，识别可观察的认识论目标（如掌握导向目标）与过程（如外包、解释寻求等）。

Result: 78.8%的学生-AI互动以非掌握目标为导向并依赖低可靠性策略；仅11.1%的互动实现掌握目标与高级策略（如认识论论证）结合。

Conclusion: EAIL框架揭示当前学生GenAI使用中的认识论短板，呼吁教学中强化掌握目标培养和可靠策略训练以提升人机协作效能。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [290] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过刘易斯信号游戏发现：记忆架构（如私有笔记本）比信道容量更能决定智能体能否建立稳定语言。无状态智能体易受高容量干扰，而外部记忆可固化交互惯例；信息瓶颈理论预测与实际结果相悖，证明冗余信道容量更利于语言演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无先验知识条件下，智能体如何通过交互历史自发形成共享语言，突破传统理论对信道容量的单一依赖，揭示记忆架构在语言演化中的作用。

Method: 在刘易斯信号游戏中测试五种记忆架构（如私有笔记本、无状态）在多变信道容量下的表现，采用大语言模型智能体进行实验，分析不同配置对协调效果的影响。

Result: 1. 私有笔记本架构在信道容量25时达到最高协调率（0.867±0.023），避免无状态智能体的高容量崩溃现象；2. 信息瓶颈理论预测的最优容量（对象数量）实际表现为脆弱点，冗余信道容量普遍更优；3. 记忆架构显著影响协调稳定性，超过信道容量本身的影响。

Conclusion: 信道容量单独无法预测智能体间的语言协调效果，记忆架构是决定能否将交互历史转化为稳定约定的关键因素；二者结合才能解释信号如何演变为语言。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [291] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0通过构建基于真实用户需求和复杂场景的评估体系，针对性解决长尾知识与复杂指令遵循问题，在推理、视觉理解及搜索能力上实现世界领先，初步展现处理复杂现实任务的能力并为海量用户提供价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂现实世界任务中的两大核心挑战：长尾知识覆盖不足与复杂指令遵循能力薄弱，同时构建符合用户真实需求的前瞻性评估体系。

Method: 以用户需求为导向设计评估系统，精选并抽象复杂场景基准测试；基于该系统针对性优化模型在长尾知识处理与多步骤指令执行方面的表现。

Result: 模型可靠性显著提升，在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力三项指标达到世界领先水平，并通过实际案例验证其初步处理复杂现实任务的能力。

Conclusion: Seed2.0标志着向解决实际复杂任务迈出的关键一步，通过满足核心用户需求和技术突破，已开始为大规模用户提供可衡量的价值增量。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [292] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP)模型，通过确定性准入控制和约束集C确保LLM生成行动的安全性，在Mnemosyne运行时中实现并验证其有效性，显著降低修复开销并保证系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型生成的工作流行动虽语法正确，但可能过时、不可行或破坏证据，导致系统安全风险。传统方法依赖生成层的能力或可信度，难以应对动态干扰，需一种机制确保行动安全且独立于提议层的可靠性。

Method: ATP将生成行动视为“提议”，经约束集C的确定性准入控制后由运行时提交；Mnemosyne通过追加式转换日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿及活跃提交记录实现ATP，并设计LCRP协议实现局部修复。证明四项安全属性（权威分离、串行等价生成准入、证据保留修复、义务隔离）及LCRP的有界修复能力。

Result: 在9个失效测试中成功拒绝目标违规，同时保留有效工作，投影与验证开销低于6%；局部修复操作量较全局重算减少一个数量级。开源代码已发布，验证可复现性。

Conclusion: ATP通过解耦生成层与运行时责任，确保系统状态正确性不受提议层可靠性影响，结合低开销验证与高效局部修复，为生成式系统提供安全可靠的执行框架。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP).
